=== FILE: ./tests/test_explanation_evaluation.py ===
import numpy as np

from efd.evaluation.explanation import (
    effective_features,
    explanation_entropy,
    evaluate_explanation_matrix,
    nonzero_feature_count,
    top_k_mass,
    top_k_overlap,
)


def test_top_k_mass():

    importance = np.array(
        [
            [0.5, 0.3, 0.2],
            [0.1, 0.2, 0.7],
        ]
    )

    result = top_k_mass(
        importance,
        1,
    )

    assert np.allclose(
        result,
        [0.5, 0.7],
    )


def test_entropy_is_nonnegative():

    importance = np.array(
        [
            [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],
            [0.5, 0.5, 0.0],
        ]
    )

    result = explanation_entropy(
        importance
    )

    assert np.all(result >= 0)

    assert np.isclose(
        result[0],
        0.0,
    )


def test_effective_features():

    importance = np.array(
        [
            [1.0, 0.0],
            [0.5, 0.5],
        ]
    )

    result = effective_features(
        importance
    )

    assert np.isclose(
        result[0],
        1.0,
    )

    assert np.isclose(
        result[1],
        2.0,
    )


def test_nonzero_feature_count():

    importance = np.array(
        [
            [0.5, 0.5, 0.0],
            [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],
        ]
    )

    result = nonzero_feature_count(
        importance
    )

    assert np.array_equal(
        result,
        [2, 1],
    )


def test_top_k_overlap():

    feature_names = [
        "A",
        "B",
        "C",
        "D",
    ]

    a = np.array(
        [
            [0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1],
        ]
    )

    b = np.array(
        [
            [0.5, 0.3, 0.1, 0.1],
        ]
    )

    result = top_k_overlap(
        a,
        b,
        feature_names,
        k=2,
    )

    assert np.isclose(
        result[0],
        1.0,
    )


def test_evaluate_explanation_matrix():

    feature_names = [
        "A",
        "B",
        "C",
    ]

    importance = np.array(
        [
            [0.5, 0.3, 0.2],
            [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],
        ]
    )

    result = evaluate_explanation_matrix(
        importance,
        feature_names,
        window=1,
    )

    assert len(result) == 2

    assert "window" in result.columns
    assert "top_1_mass" in result.columns
    assert "top_5_mass" in result.columns
    assert "entropy" in result.columns
    assert "effective_features" in result.columns

    assert np.isclose(
        result.iloc[1]["top_1_mass"],
        1.0,
    )
=== FILE: ./tests/test_exiffi_temporal_evaluation.py ===
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from scripts.evaluate_exiffi_temporal_explanations import (
    build_class_summary,
    build_summary,
)


def make_metrics():

    return pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "window": [1, 1, 2, 2],
            "isFraud": [0, 1, 0, 1],
            "n_features": [4, 4, 4, 4],
            "top_1_mass": [
                0.4,
                0.5,
                0.3,
                0.6,
            ],
            "top_5_mass": [
                0.7,
                0.8,
                0.6,
                0.9,
            ],
            "top_10_mass": [
                0.9,
                0.95,
                0.85,
                0.98,
            ],
            "entropy": [
                1.0,
                1.2,
                1.1,
                1.3,
            ],
            "effective_features": [
                2.0,
                2.5,
                2.2,
                2.8,
            ],
            "nonzero_features": [
                4,
                4,
                4,
                4,
            ],
        }
    )


def test_build_summary_has_one_row_per_window():

    metrics = make_metrics()

    summary = build_summary(metrics)

    assert len(summary) == 2

    assert summary["window"].tolist() == [
        1,
        2,
    ]


def test_build_summary_preserves_class_counts():

    metrics = make_metrics()

    summary = build_summary(metrics)

    assert summary.loc[
        summary["window"] == 1,
        "n_fraud",
    ].iloc[0] == 1

    assert summary.loc[
        summary["window"] == 1,
        "n_nonfraud",
    ].iloc[0] == 1


def test_build_summary_calculates_top_k_mass():

    metrics = make_metrics()

    summary = build_summary(metrics)

    value = summary.loc[
        summary["window"] == 1,
        "mean_top_1_mass",
    ].iloc[0]

    assert np.isclose(
        value,
        0.45,
    )


def test_class_summary_contains_fraud_and_nonfraud():

    metrics = make_metrics()

    summary = build_class_summary(metrics)

    assert len(summary) == 4

    assert set(
        summary["class"]
    ) == {
        "fraud",
        "nonfraud",
    }
=== FILE: ./tests/test_explanation_fidelity_control.py ===
import numpy as np

from efd.evaluation.explanation_fidelity import (
    evaluate_random_fidelity_matrix,
)


def test_random_fidelity_returns_expected_rows():

    from sklearn.ensemble import IsolationForest

    rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    X_train = rng.normal(
        size=(100, 5)
    )

    X = rng.normal(
        size=(3, 5)
    )

    model = IsolationForest(
        n_estimators=10,
        random_state=42,
    )

    model.fit(X_train)

    class Explanation:

        def __init__(self, index):
            self.sample_index = index

    explanations = [
        Explanation(i)
        for i in range(len(X))
    ]

    baseline = np.median(
        X_train,
        axis=0,
    )

    results = evaluate_random_fidelity_matrix(
        model=model,
        X=X,
        explanations=explanations,
        baseline=baseline,
        k_values=[1, 2],
        random_state=42,
    )

    assert len(results) == 6

    assert set(
        results["top_k"]
    ) == {1, 2}

    assert (
        results["score_change"]
        .notna()
        .all()
    )
=== FILE: ./tests/test_isolation_forest.py ===
import numpy as np

from efd.models.isolation_forest import (
    IsolationForestDetector,
)


def test_isolation_forest_score_direction():

    rng = np.random.RandomState(42)

    normal = rng.normal(
        loc=0,
        scale=1,
        size=(100, 3),
    )

    anomalies = rng.normal(
        loc=10,
        scale=1,
        size=(5, 3),
    )

    x = np.vstack(
        [
            normal,
            anomalies,
        ]
    )

    detector = (
        IsolationForestDetector(
            n_estimators=50,
            random_state=42,
        )
    )

    detector.fit(x)

    scores = (
        detector
        .anomaly_score(x)
    )

    assert scores.shape == (105,)

    # On average, the deliberately
    # distant observations should have
    # higher anomaly scores.
    assert (
        scores[-5:].mean()
        > scores[:100].mean()
    )
=== FILE: ./tests/test_temporal.py ===
import pandas as pd

from efd.data.temporal import chronological_windows


def test_chronological_windows():

    df = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "TransactionDT": [
                50,
                10,
                40,
                20,
                30,
                60,
                70,
                80,
                90,
                100,
            ],
            "isFraud": [
                0,
                0,
                1,
                0,
                0,
                1,
                0,
                1,
                0,
                0,
            ],
        }
    )

    train, windows = chronological_windows(
        df,
        time_col="TransactionDT",
        train_fraction=0.6,
        n_future_windows=2,
    )

    assert (
        train["TransactionDT"]
        .tolist()
        == [10, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60]
    )

    assert (
        windows[0]["TransactionDT"]
        .tolist()
        == [70, 80]
    )

    assert (
        windows[1]["TransactionDT"]
        .tolist()
        == [90, 100]
    )
=== FILE: ./tests/test_exiffi.py ===
def test_exiffi_uses_exiffi_path_weight():
    import numpy as np
    from sklearn.ensemble import IsolationForest

    from efd.explanations.exiffi import ExIFFI

    rng = np.random.RandomState(42)

    X = rng.normal(size=(100, 4))

    model = IsolationForest(
        n_estimators=1,
        max_samples=32,
        random_state=42,
    )

    model.fit(X)

    feature_names = [
        "f0",
        "f1",
        "f2",
        "f3",
    ]

    explainer = ExIFFI(
        model=model,
        feature_names=feature_names,
    )

    x = X[0]

    explanation = explainer.explain(x)

    tree = model.estimators_[0].tree_

    indicator = model.estimators_[0].decision_path(
        x.reshape(1, -1)
    )

    path_nodes = indicator.indices[
        indicator.indptr[0]:
        indicator.indptr[1]
    ]

    expected = np.zeros(
        X.shape[1],
        dtype=float,
    )

    for node_id in path_nodes:

        feature = tree.feature[node_id]

        if feature < 0:
            continue

        left = tree.children_left[node_id]
        right = tree.children_right[node_id]

        if x[feature] <= tree.threshold[node_id]:
            child = left
        else:
            child = right

        n_parent = tree.n_node_samples[node_id]
        n_child = tree.n_node_samples[child]

        expected[feature] += (
            n_parent / n_child
        )

    actual = explanation.contributions

    assert np.allclose(
        actual,
        expected,
    )

=== FILE: ./tests/test_preprocessing.py ===
import pandas as pd

from efd.data.preprocessing import (
    NumericTransactionPreprocessor,
)


def test_preprocessing_uses_training_schema_only():

    train = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "TransactionID": [1, 2, 3, 4],
            "TransactionDT": [1, 2, 3, 4],
            "isFraud": [0, 0, 1, 0],

            "feature_a": [
                1.0,
                None,
                3.0,
                4.0,
            ],

            "constant": [
                1,
                1,
                1,
                1,
            ],

            "text_feature": [
                "a",
                "b",
                "a",
                "b",
            ],
        }
    )

    future = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "TransactionID": [5],
            "TransactionDT": [5],
            "isFraud": [1],

            "feature_a": [None],

            "constant": [1],

            "text_feature": ["future"],
        }
    )

    processor = (
        NumericTransactionPreprocessor()
    )

    x_train = (
        processor
        .fit_transform(train)
    )

    x_future = (
        processor
        .transform(future)
    )

    assert (
        processor.feature_names_
        == ["feature_a"]
    )

    assert x_train.shape == (4, 1)

    assert x_future.shape == (1, 1)

    # Future missing value must use
    # the training median.
    assert (
        x_future.iloc[0, 0]
        == x_train["feature_a"].median()
    )
=== FILE: ./tests/test_explanation_drift.py ===
import numpy as np

from efd.evaluation.explanation_drift import (
    cosine_distance,
    explanation_drift_summary,
    feature_drift_table,
    js_divergence,
    l1_distance,
    l2_distance,
    rank_correlation,
    top_k_jaccard,
)


def test_identical_matrices_have_zero_drift():

    X = np.array(
        [
            [0.1, 0.2, 0.7],
            [0.2, 0.2, 0.6],
        ]
    )

    assert cosine_distance(
        X,
        X,
    ) == 0.0

    assert l1_distance(
        X,
        X,
    ) == 0.0

    assert l2_distance(
        X,
        X,
    ) == 0.0

    assert js_divergence(
        X,
        X,
    ) == 0.0

    assert top_k_jaccard(
        X,
        X,
        2,
    ) == 1.0

    assert rank_correlation(
        X,
        X,
    ) == 1.0


def test_changed_explanations_have_positive_drift():

    X_a = np.array(
        [
            [0.8, 0.1, 0.1],
            [0.7, 0.2, 0.1],
        ]
    )

    X_b = np.array(
        [
            [0.1, 0.8, 0.1],
            [0.1, 0.7, 0.2],
        ]
    )

    assert js_divergence(
        X_a,
        X_b,
    ) > 0

    assert l1_distance(
        X_a,
        X_b,
    ) > 0

    assert l2_distance(
        X_a,
        X_b,
    ) > 0


def test_feature_drift_table():

    X_a = np.array(
        [
            [0.8, 0.1, 0.1],
            [0.7, 0.2, 0.1],
        ]
    )

    X_b = np.array(
        [
            [0.1, 0.8, 0.1],
            [0.1, 0.7, 0.2],
        ]
    )

    result = feature_drift_table(
        X_a,
        X_b,
        ["a", "b", "c"],
        "w1",
        "w2",
    )

    assert len(result) == 3

    assert (
        "importance_change"
        in result.columns
    )

    assert (
        "rank_change"
        in result.columns
    )


def test_summary_contains_expected_metrics():

    X_a = np.array(
        [[0.8, 0.1, 0.1]]
    )

    X_b = np.array(
        [[0.1, 0.8, 0.1]]
    )

    result = explanation_drift_summary(
        X_a,
        X_b,
        "w1",
        "w2",
    )

    assert (
        result["comparison"]
        == "w1_w2"
    )

    assert (
        result["js_divergence"]
        > 0
    )

    assert (
        result["l1_distance"]
        > 0
    )

    assert (
        result["l2_distance"]
        > 0
    )
=== FILE: ./tests/test_explanation_fidelity.py ===
import numpy as np

from efd.evaluation.explanation_fidelity import (
    perturb_features,
)


def test_perturb_features_changes_only_selected_features():

    x = np.array(
        [1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0]
    )

    baseline = np.array(
        [10.0, 20.0, 30.0, 40.0]
    )

    indices = np.array(
        [1, 3]
    )

    result = perturb_features(
        x=x,
        feature_indices=indices,
        baseline=baseline,
    )

    expected = np.array(
        [1.0, 20.0, 3.0, 40.0]
    )

    assert np.allclose(
        result,
        expected,
    )


def test_perturb_features_does_not_modify_original():

    x = np.array(
        [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]
    )

    baseline = np.array(
        [10.0, 20.0, 30.0]
    )

    perturb_features(
        x=x,
        feature_indices=np.array([0]),
        baseline=baseline,
    )

    assert np.allclose(
        x,
        [1.0, 2.0, 3.0],
    )

=== FILE: ./scripts/audit_ieee_cis.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"))

from efd.data.audit import identity_join_coverage, scan_transactions, transaction_schema, write_audit


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--transactions", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--identity")
    p.add_argument("--output", default="results/audit")
    p.add_argument("--chunksize", type=int, default=100_000)
    p.add_argument("--time-bins", type=int, default=20)
    args = p.parse_args()

    audit = scan_transactions(args.transactions, args.chunksize, args.time_bins)
    audit["schema"] = transaction_schema(args.transactions)
    if args.identity:
        audit["identity_join"] = identity_join_coverage(args.transactions, args.identity, args.chunksize)
    write_audit(audit, args.output)

    print("\nIEEE-CIS audit complete")
    print(f"Rows: {audit['rows']:,}")
    print(f"Columns: {audit['columns']:,}")
    print(f"Fraud: {audit['fraud_count']:,} ({audit['fraud_rate']:.4%})")
    print(f"TransactionDT: {audit['transaction_dt_min']} -> {audit['transaction_dt_max']}")
    if "identity_join" in audit:
        print(f"Identity coverage: {audit['identity_join']['matched_rate']:.2%}")
    print(f"Results: {Path(args.output).resolve()}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

=== FILE: ./scripts/evaluate_exiffi_temporal_explanations.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from efd.evaluation.explanation import (
    evaluate_explanation_matrix,
)


INPUT_DIR = Path("results/explanations")

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("results/tables")

OUTPUT = (
    OUTPUT_DIR
    / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_explanation_metrics.csv"
)

SUMMARY_OUTPUT = (
    OUTPUT_DIR
    / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_explanation_summary.csv"
)

CLASS_SUMMARY_OUTPUT = (
    OUTPUT_DIR
    / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_explanation_class_summary.csv"
)


def _window_input_path(window: int) -> Path:
    return (
        INPUT_DIR
        / f"static_if_exiffi_w{window}_sample.csv"
    )


def _extract_feature_names(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> list[str]:

    importance_cols = [
        column
        for column in df.columns
        if column.startswith("importance__")
    ]

    if not importance_cols:
        raise ValueError(
            "No importance columns found."
        )

    return [
        column.replace(
            "importance__",
            "",
            1,
        )
        for column in importance_cols
    ]


def build_summary(
    metrics: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Build overall temporal explanation summary.

    The summary preserves fraud and non-fraud class counts
    for every temporal window.
    """

    required_columns = {
        "window",
        "isFraud",
        "top_1_mass",
        "top_5_mass",
        "top_10_mass",
        "entropy",
        "effective_features",
        "nonzero_features",
    }

    missing = (
        required_columns
        - set(metrics.columns)
    )

    if missing:
        raise ValueError(
            "Missing required columns for temporal "
            "summary: "
            f"{sorted(missing)}"
        )

    rows = []

    for window, group in metrics.groupby(
        "window"
    ):

        n_observations = len(group)

        n_fraud = int(
            (
                group["isFraud"] == 1
            ).sum()
        )

        n_nonfraud = int(
            (
                group["isFraud"] == 0
            ).sum()
        )

        fraud_rate = (
            n_fraud / n_observations
            if n_observations > 0
            else 0.0
        )

        if "n_features" in group.columns:
            n_features = int(
                group["n_features"].iloc[0]
            )
        else:
            n_features = None

        rows.append(
            {
                "window": window,

                "n_features": n_features,

                "n_observations": (
                    n_observations
                ),

                "n_fraud": n_fraud,

                "n_nonfraud": n_nonfraud,

                "fraud_rate": fraud_rate,

                "mean_top_1_mass": (
                    group["top_1_mass"].mean()
                ),

                "mean_top_5_mass": (
                    group["top_5_mass"].mean()
                ),

                "mean_top_10_mass": (
                    group["top_10_mass"].mean()
                ),

                "mean_entropy": (
                    group["entropy"].mean()
                ),

                "mean_effective_features": (
                    group[
                        "effective_features"
                    ].mean()
                ),

                "mean_nonzero_features": (
                    group[
                        "nonzero_features"
                    ].mean()
                ),

                "median_top_1_mass": (
                    group["top_1_mass"].median()
                ),

                "median_top_5_mass": (
                    group["top_5_mass"].median()
                ),

                "median_top_10_mass": (
                    group["top_10_mass"].median()
                ),

                "median_entropy": (
                    group["entropy"].median()
                ),

                "median_effective_features": (
                    group[
                        "effective_features"
                    ].median()
                ),

                "median_nonzero_features": (
                    group[
                        "nonzero_features"
                    ].median()
                ),

                "n_samples": float(
                    n_observations
                ),
            }
        )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(rows)
        .sort_values("window")
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


def build_class_summary(
    metrics: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Build class-stratified temporal explanation metrics.

    Produces one row for each:

        window × class

    where class is either:

        fraud
        nonfraud
    """

    required_columns = {
        "window",
        "isFraud",
        "top_1_mass",
        "top_5_mass",
        "top_10_mass",
        "entropy",
        "effective_features",
        "nonzero_features",
    }

    missing = (
        required_columns
        - set(metrics.columns)
    )

    if missing:
        raise ValueError(
            "Missing required columns for class "
            "summary: "
            f"{sorted(missing)}"
        )

    grouped = (
        metrics
        .groupby(
            ["window", "isFraud"],
            dropna=False,
        )
        .agg(
            n_observations=(
                "isFraud",
                "count",
            ),

            mean_top_1_mass=(
                "top_1_mass",
                "mean",
            ),

            mean_top_5_mass=(
                "top_5_mass",
                "mean",
            ),

            mean_top_10_mass=(
                "top_10_mass",
                "mean",
            ),

            mean_entropy=(
                "entropy",
                "mean",
            ),

            mean_effective_features=(
                "effective_features",
                "mean",
            ),

            mean_nonzero_features=(
                "nonzero_features",
                "mean",
            ),

            median_top_1_mass=(
                "top_1_mass",
                "median",
            ),

            median_top_5_mass=(
                "top_5_mass",
                "median",
            ),

            median_top_10_mass=(
                "top_10_mass",
                "median",
            ),

            median_entropy=(
                "entropy",
                "median",
            ),

            median_effective_features=(
                "effective_features",
                "median",
            ),

            median_nonzero_features=(
                "nonzero_features",
                "median",
            ),
        )
        .reset_index()
    )

    grouped["class"] = grouped[
        "isFraud"
    ].map(
        {
            0: "nonfraud",
            1: "fraud",
        }
    )

    grouped = grouped[
        [
            "window",
            "isFraud",
            "class",
            "n_observations",

            "mean_top_1_mass",
            "mean_top_5_mass",
            "mean_top_10_mass",

            "mean_entropy",
            "mean_effective_features",
            "mean_nonzero_features",

            "median_top_1_mass",
            "median_top_5_mass",
            "median_top_10_mass",

            "median_entropy",
            "median_effective_features",
            "median_nonzero_features",
        ]
    ]

    return (
        grouped
        .sort_values(
            [
                "window",
                "isFraud",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


def evaluate_window(
    window: int,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    input_path = _window_input_path(
        window
    )

    if not input_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Explanation file not found: "
            f"{input_path}"
        )

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"Window {window}")
    print("=" * 70)

    df = pd.read_csv(
        input_path
    )

    if "isFraud" not in df.columns:
        raise ValueError(
            f"'isFraud' column missing from "
            f"{input_path}"
        )

    if "anomaly_score" not in df.columns:
        raise ValueError(
            f"'anomaly_score' column missing from "
            f"{input_path}"
        )

    feature_names = _extract_feature_names(
        df
    )

    importance_cols = [
        f"importance__{feature}"
        for feature in feature_names
    ]

    importance = (
        df[importance_cols]
        .to_numpy(dtype=float)
    )

    print(
        f"  Observations: {len(df):,}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Features: {len(feature_names):,}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Fraud: "
        f"{int(df['isFraud'].sum()):,}"
    )

    metrics = evaluate_explanation_matrix(
        importance=importance,
        feature_names=feature_names,
        window=window,
    )

    # Preserve observation-level information
    # from the explanation output.
    metrics.insert(
        2,
        "isFraud",
        df["isFraud"].to_numpy(),
    )

    metrics.insert(
        3,
        "anomaly_score",
        df["anomaly_score"].to_numpy(),
    )

    # Preserve feature count at observation level
    # so temporal summaries can validate the
    # preprocessing schema.
    metrics["n_features"] = (
        len(feature_names)
    )

    # Preserve sample count.
    metrics["n_samples"] = (
        len(df)
    )

    print(
        "  Evaluation complete."
    )

    return metrics


def main():

    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "BASELINE 1 — TEMPORAL EXIFFI "
        "EXPLANATION EVALUATION"
    )
    print("=" * 70)

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Evaluate all temporal windows
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    window_metrics = []

    for window in range(1, 5):

        metrics = evaluate_window(
            window
        )

        window_metrics.append(
            metrics
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Combine observation-level metrics
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print(
        "Combining temporal results..."
    )

    combined_metrics = pd.concat(
        window_metrics,
        ignore_index=True,
    )

    combined_metrics.to_csv(
        OUTPUT,
        index=False,
    )

    print()
    print(
        "Observation-level metrics saved to:"
    )

    print(
        OUTPUT
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Overall temporal summary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print(
        "Creating temporal summary..."
    )

    summary_df = build_summary(
        combined_metrics
    )

    summary_df.to_csv(
        SUMMARY_OUTPUT,
        index=False,
    )

    print()
    print(
        "Temporal summary saved to:"
    )

    print(
        SUMMARY_OUTPUT
    )

    print()
    print(
        "Temporal explanation summary:"
    )

    print()

    print(
        summary_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Class-stratified temporal summary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print(
        "Creating class-stratified "
        "temporal summary..."
    )

    class_summary_df = (
        build_class_summary(
            combined_metrics
        )
    )

    class_summary_df.to_csv(
        CLASS_SUMMARY_OUTPUT,
        index=False,
    )

    print()
    print(
        "Class-stratified temporal summary saved to:"
    )

    print(
        CLASS_SUMMARY_OUTPUT
    )

    print()
    print(
        "Class-stratified temporal summary:"
    )

    print()

    print(
        class_summary_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Validation
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "TEMPORAL EVALUATION VALIDATION"
    )
    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        f"Windows evaluated: "
        f"{combined_metrics['window'].nunique()}"
    )

    print(
        f"Total observations: "
        f"{len(combined_metrics):,}"
    )

    print(
        "Feature counts:"
    )

    print(
        summary_df[
            [
                "window",
                "n_features",
            ]
        ].to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print()
    print(
        "Class counts:"
    )

    print(
        summary_df[
            [
                "window",
                "n_fraud",
                "n_nonfraud",
                "fraud_rate",
            ]
        ].to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Final validation checks
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    expected_windows = {
        1,
        2,
        3,
        4,
    }

    actual_windows = set(
        combined_metrics[
            "window"
        ].unique()
    )

    if actual_windows != expected_windows:
        raise ValueError(
            "Unexpected temporal windows. "
            f"Expected {expected_windows}, "
            f"got {actual_windows}"
        )

    feature_counts = (
        summary_df[
            "n_features"
        ]
        .dropna()
        .astype(int)
        .unique()
    )

    if len(feature_counts) != 1:
        raise ValueError(
            "Feature count changed across "
            "temporal windows: "
            f"{feature_counts.tolist()}"
        )

    for window in expected_windows:

        row = summary_df.loc[
            summary_df["window"] == window
        ]

        if row.empty:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Missing summary for window "
                f"{window}"
            )

        n_observations = int(
            row["n_observations"].iloc[0]
        )

        n_fraud = int(
            row["n_fraud"].iloc[0]
        )

        n_nonfraud = int(
            row["n_nonfraud"].iloc[0]
        )

        if (
            n_fraud
            + n_nonfraud
            != n_observations
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"Class counts do not sum to "
                f"observations for window "
                f"{window}."
            )

    print()
    print(
        "Temporal ExIFFI evaluation complete."
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
=== FILE: ./scripts/evaluate_exiffi_explanations.py ===
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from efd.evaluation.explanation import (
    evaluate_explanation_matrix,
    summarize_explanation_metrics,
)


EXPLANATION_DIR = Path(
    "results/explanations"
)

OUTPUT_DIR = Path(
    "results/tables"
)

N_WINDOWS = 4


def load_explanation_file(
    window: int,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, list[str], pd.DataFrame]:
    """
    Load one ExIFFI explanation file and extract
    the importance matrix.
    """

    input_path = (
        EXPLANATION_DIR
        / f"static_if_exiffi_w{window}_sample.csv"
    )

    if not input_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Explanation file not found:\n"
            f"{input_path}"
        )

    df = pd.read_csv(
        input_path
    )

    importance_cols = [
        column
        for column in df.columns
        if column.startswith("importance__")
    ]

    if not importance_cols:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No importance columns found in:\n"
            f"{input_path}"
        )

    feature_names = [
        column.replace(
            "importance__",
            "",
            1,
        )
        for column in importance_cols
    ]

    importance = (
        df[importance_cols]
        .to_numpy(dtype=float)
    )

    return (
        df,
        feature_names,
        importance,
    )


def evaluate_window(
    window: int,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    """
    Evaluate ExIFFI explanations for one temporal window.

    Returns
    -------
    metrics:
        Observation-level explanation metrics.

    summary_df:
        Window-level summary metrics.
    """

    print(
        f"\n{'-' * 70}"
    )

    print(
        f"WINDOW {window}"
    )

    print(
        f"{'-' * 70}"
    )

    print(
        "\nLoading explanation output..."
    )

    df, feature_names, importance = (
        load_explanation_file(
            window
        )
    )

    print(
        f"Observations: {len(df):,}"
    )

    print(
        f"Features: {len(feature_names):,}"
    )

    print(
        f"Importance matrix: "
        f"{importance.shape}"
    )

    print(
        "\nEvaluating explanations..."
    )

    metrics = evaluate_explanation_matrix(
        importance=importance,
        feature_names=feature_names,
        window=window,
    )

    # Preserve the original model information.
    metrics.insert(
        2,
        "isFraud",
        df["isFraud"].to_numpy(),
    )

    metrics.insert(
        3,
        "anomaly_score",
        df["anomaly_score"].to_numpy(),
    )

    output_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / f"static_if_exiffi_w{window}_"
          "explanation_metrics.csv"
    )

    metrics.to_csv(
        output_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"\nObservation-level metrics saved to:"
        f"\n{output_path}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Summary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    summary = summarize_explanation_metrics(
        metrics
    )

    summary_df = (
        summary
        .to_frame()
        .T
    )

    summary_df.insert(
        0,
        "window",
        window,
    )

    summary_df.insert(
        1,
        "n_features",
        len(feature_names),
    )

    summary_df.insert(
        2,
        "n_observations",
        len(df),
    )

    summary_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / f"static_if_exiffi_w{window}_"
          "explanation_summary.csv"
    )

    summary_df.to_csv(
        summary_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"\nSummary saved to:"
        f"\n{summary_path}"
    )

    print(
        "\nWindow summary:"
    )

    print(
        summary_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    return (
        metrics,
        summary_df,
    )


def main():

    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "BASELINE 1 — EXIFFI "
        "TEMPORAL EXPLANATION EVALUATION"
    )
    print("=" * 70)

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    all_metrics = []
    all_summaries = []

    for window in range(
        1,
        N_WINDOWS + 1,
    ):

        metrics, summary = (
            evaluate_window(
                window
            )
        )

        all_metrics.append(
            metrics
        )

        all_summaries.append(
            summary
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Combined observation-level results
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n"
        + "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "CREATING COMBINED TEMPORAL RESULTS"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 70
    )

    combined_metrics = pd.concat(
        all_metrics,
        ignore_index=True,
    )

    combined_metrics_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_explanation_metrics.csv"
    )

    combined_metrics.to_csv(
        combined_metrics_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"\nCombined observation-level metrics saved to:"
        f"\n{combined_metrics_path}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Combined summary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    combined_summary = pd.concat(
        all_summaries,
        ignore_index=True,
    )

    combined_summary_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_explanation_summary.csv"
    )

    combined_summary.to_csv(
        combined_summary_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"\nCombined temporal summary saved to:"
        f"\n{combined_summary_path}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Display temporal comparison
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n"
        + "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "TEMPORAL EXPLANATION SUMMARY"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 70
    )

    print()

    print(
        combined_summary.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nDone."
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Runnable project scripts that also expose reusable helpers for tests."""

=== FILE: ./scripts/evaluate_exiffi_random_control.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from efd.data.preprocessing import (
    FeaturePolicy,
    NumericTransactionPreprocessor,
)

from efd.data.temporal import (
    chronological_windows,
)

from efd.models.isolation_forest import (
    IsolationForestDetector,
)

from efd.explanations.exiffi import (
    ExIFFI,
)

from efd.evaluation.explanation_fidelity import (
    evaluate_fidelity_matrix,
    evaluate_random_fidelity_matrix,
)


def run_experiment(
    transactions_path: str,
    train_fraction: float = 0.60,
    n_estimators: int = 300,
    random_state: int = 42,
    sample_size: int = 100,
):

    print("=" * 70)
    print("BASELINE 1 — EXIFFI VS RANDOM FIDELITY CONTROL")
    print("=" * 70)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Load data
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print("\n[1/8] Loading transaction dataset...")

    df = pd.read_csv(
        transactions_path,
        low_memory=False,
    )

    print(
        f"Loaded {len(df):,} transactions."
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Chronological split
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[2/8] Creating chronological split..."
    )

    train, future_windows = chronological_windows(
        df,
        time_col="TransactionDT",
        train_fraction=train_fraction,
        n_future_windows=1,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Preprocessing
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[3/8] Fitting preprocessing..."
    )

    preprocessor = NumericTransactionPreprocessor(
        policy=FeaturePolicy(
            target="isFraud",
            temporal="TransactionDT",
            identifier="TransactionID",
            max_missing_rate=0.95,
        )
    )

    x_train = (
        preprocessor
        .fit_transform(train)
    )

    feature_names = (
        preprocessor.feature_names_
    )

    print(
        f"Features: {len(feature_names)}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Train model
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[4/8] Training Isolation Forest..."
    )

    detector = IsolationForestDetector(
        n_estimators=n_estimators,
        max_samples="auto",
        contamination="auto",
        random_state=random_state,
        n_jobs=-1,
    )

    detector.fit(x_train)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Generate explanations
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[5/8] Generating ExIFFI explanations..."
    )

    explainer = ExIFFI(
        model=detector.model,
        feature_names=feature_names,
    )

    window = future_windows[0]

    X_window = (
        preprocessor
        .transform(window)
    )

    n = min(
        sample_size,
        len(X_window),
    )

    X_sample = X_window.iloc[:n].to_numpy(
        dtype=float,
    )

    explanations = [
        explainer.explain(
            X_sample[i],
            sample_index=i,
        )
        for i in range(n)
    ]

    print(
        f"Generated {n} explanations."
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Baseline
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[6/8] Calculating training baseline..."
    )

    baseline = np.median(
        x_train,
        axis=0,
    )

    k_values = [
        1,
        3,
        5,
        10,
        20,
    ]

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Evaluate both conditions
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[7/8] Evaluating ExIFFI and random control..."
    )

    exiffi = evaluate_fidelity_matrix(
        model=detector.model,
        X=X_sample,
        explanations=explanations,
        baseline=baseline,
        k_values=k_values,
    )

    exiffi_df = pd.DataFrame(
        [
            {
                "sample_index": r.sample_index,
                "top_k": r.top_k,
                "score_change": r.score_change,
                "relative_score_change": (
                    r.relative_score_change
                ),
            }
            for r in exiffi
        ]
    )

    exiffi_df["condition"] = "exiffi"

    random_df = evaluate_random_fidelity_matrix(
        model=detector.model,
        X=X_sample,
        explanations=explanations,
        baseline=baseline,
        k_values=k_values,
        random_state=random_state,
    )

    random_df["condition"] = "random"

    comparison = pd.concat(
        [
            exiffi_df,
            random_df[
                [
                    "sample_index",
                    "top_k",
                    "score_change",
                    "relative_score_change",
                    "condition",
                ]
            ],
        ],
        ignore_index=True,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Save results
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[8/8] Saving results..."
    )

    output_dir = Path(
        "results/tables"
    )

    output_dir.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    output_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_vs_random_fidelity.csv"
    )

    comparison.to_csv(
        output_path,
        index=False,
    )

    summary = (
        comparison
        .groupby(
            [
                "condition",
                "top_k",
            ]
        )
        .agg(
            n_observations=(
                "sample_index",
                "count",
            ),
            mean_score_change=(
                "score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_score_change=(
                "score_change",
                "median",
            ),
            mean_relative_score_change=(
                "relative_score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_relative_score_change=(
                "relative_score_change",
                "median",
            ),
        )
        .reset_index()
    )

    summary_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_vs_random_fidelity_summary.csv"
    )

    summary.to_csv(
        summary_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"\nResults saved to:"
        f"\n{output_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"\nSummary saved to:"
        f"\n{summary_path}"
    )

    print("\nFidelity comparison:")
    print()

    print(
        summary.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    return comparison, summary


def main():

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--transactions",
        required=True,
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--sample-size",
        type=int,
        default=100,
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    run_experiment(
        transactions_path=args.transactions,
        sample_size=args.sample_size,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

=== FILE: ./scripts/evaluate_exiffi_temporal_drift_significance.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy.spatial.distance import cosine
from scipy.stats import spearmanr
from scipy.stats import false_discovery_control


# ============================================================
# Configuration
# ============================================================

DEFAULT_INPUT_DIR = Path("results/explanations")
DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR = Path("results/tables")

WINDOWS = [1, 2, 3, 4]

TOP_K_VALUES = [1, 5, 10, 20]

DEFAULT_N_BOOTSTRAP = 1000
DEFAULT_N_PERMUTATIONS = 1000
DEFAULT_RANDOM_STATE = 42

ALPHA = 0.05


# ============================================================
# Loading
# ============================================================


def load_window(
    window: int,
    input_dir: Path,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    path = (
        input_dir
        / f"static_if_exiffi_w{window}_sample.csv"
    )

    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Missing temporal explanation file:\n{path}"
        )

    df = pd.read_csv(path)

    importance_cols = [
        c
        for c in df.columns
        if c.startswith("importance__")
    ]

    if not importance_cols:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No importance columns found in {path}"
        )

    print(
        f"Window {window}: "
        f"{len(df):,} observations, "
        f"{len(importance_cols):,} features"
    )

    return df


def extract_importance_matrix(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, List[str]]:

    importance_cols = [
        c
        for c in df.columns
        if c.startswith("importance__")
    ]

    feature_names = [
        c.replace(
            "importance__",
            "",
            1,
        )
        for c in importance_cols
    ]

    X = df[
        importance_cols
    ].to_numpy(dtype=float)

    return X, feature_names


# ============================================================
# Normalisation
# ============================================================


def normalize_explanations(
    X: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:

    X = np.abs(X)

    row_sums = X.sum(
        axis=1,
        keepdims=True,
    )

    row_sums[
        row_sums == 0
    ] = 1.0

    return X / row_sums


# ============================================================
# Distribution-level metrics
# ============================================================


def mean_explanation_distribution(
    X: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:

    X_norm = normalize_explanations(X)

    distribution = X_norm.mean(
        axis=0
    )

    total = distribution.sum()

    if total > 0:
        distribution = (
            distribution / total
        )

    return distribution


def js_divergence(
    p: np.ndarray,
    q: np.ndarray,
) -> float:

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float)

    p = p / max(p.sum(), 1e-12)
    q = q / max(q.sum(), 1e-12)

    m = 0.5 * (p + q)

    def kl(a, b):

        mask = a > 0

        return np.sum(
            a[mask]
            * np.log2(
                a[mask]
                / np.maximum(
                    b[mask],
                    1e-12,
                )
            )
        )

    return float(
        0.5 * kl(p, m)
        + 0.5 * kl(q, m)
    )


def cosine_distance(
    p: np.ndarray,
    q: np.ndarray,
) -> float:

    if (
        np.linalg.norm(p) == 0
        or np.linalg.norm(q) == 0
    ):
        return 0.0

    return float(
        cosine(p, q)
    )


def l1_distance(
    p: np.ndarray,
    q: np.ndarray,
) -> float:

    return float(
        np.sum(
            np.abs(p - q)
        )
    )


def l2_distance(
    p: np.ndarray,
    q: np.ndarray,
) -> float:

    return float(
        np.linalg.norm(
            p - q
        )
    )


def rank_correlation(
    p: np.ndarray,
    q: np.ndarray,
) -> float:

    result = spearmanr(
        p,
        q,
    )

    value = result.statistic

    if np.isnan(value):
        return 0.0

    return float(value)


# ============================================================
# Top-K
# ============================================================


def top_k_features(
    distribution: np.ndarray,
    k: int,
) -> set:

    k = min(
        k,
        len(distribution),
    )

    indices = np.argsort(
        distribution
    )[::-1][:k]

    return set(
        indices.tolist()
    )


def jaccard_similarity(
    a: set,
    b: set,
) -> float:

    union = a | b

    if not union:
        return 1.0

    return len(a & b) / len(union)


# ============================================================
# Core drift calculation
# ============================================================


def calculate_drift(
    X_a: np.ndarray,
    X_b: np.ndarray,
) -> Dict[str, float]:

    p = mean_explanation_distribution(
        X_a
    )

    q = mean_explanation_distribution(
        X_b
    )

    return {
        "js_divergence": js_divergence(
            p,
            q,
        ),
        "cosine_distance": cosine_distance(
            p,
            q,
        ),
        "l1_distance": l1_distance(
            p,
            q,
        ),
        "l2_distance": l2_distance(
            p,
            q,
        ),
        "spearman_rank_correlation":
            rank_correlation(
                p,
                q,
            ),
    }


# ============================================================
# Bootstrap
# ============================================================


def bootstrap_drift(
    X_a: np.ndarray,
    X_b: np.ndarray,
    metric: str,
    n_bootstrap: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> Tuple[
    float,
    float,
    float,
    float,
    float,
]:

    n_a = len(X_a)
    n_b = len(X_b)

    observed = calculate_drift(
        X_a,
        X_b,
    )[metric]

    values = np.empty(
        n_bootstrap,
        dtype=float,
    )

    for i in range(
        n_bootstrap
    ):

        idx_a = rng.integers(
            0,
            n_a,
            size=n_a,
        )

        idx_b = rng.integers(
            0,
            n_b,
            size=n_b,
        )

        result = calculate_drift(
            X_a[idx_a],
            X_b[idx_b],
        )

        values[i] = result[
            metric
        ]

    lower = np.percentile(
        values,
        2.5,
    )

    upper = np.percentile(
        values,
        97.5,
    )

    bootstrap_mean = float(
        np.mean(values)
    )

    bootstrap_bias = (
        bootstrap_mean
        - observed
    )

    # Fraction of bootstrap statistics
    # smaller than the observed statistic.
    percentile_position = float(
        np.mean(
            values <= observed
        )
    )

    return (
        float(observed),
        float(lower),
        float(upper),
        bootstrap_bias,
        percentile_position,
    )


# ============================================================
# Permutation testing
# ============================================================


def permutation_test(
    X_a: np.ndarray,
    X_b: np.ndarray,
    metric: str,
    n_permutations: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> Tuple[float, float]:

    observed = calculate_drift(
        X_a,
        X_b,
    )[metric]

    combined = np.vstack(
        [
            X_a,
            X_b,
        ]
    )

    n_a = len(X_a)

    null_values = np.empty(
        n_permutations,
        dtype=float,
    )

    for i in range(
        n_permutations
    ):

        indices = rng.permutation(
            len(combined)
        )

        perm_a = combined[
            indices[:n_a]
        ]

        perm_b = combined[
            indices[n_a:]
        ]

        null_values[i] = (
            calculate_drift(
                perm_a,
                perm_b,
            )[metric]
        )

    if metric == "spearman_rank_correlation":

        observed_drift = (
            1.0 - observed
        )

        null_drift = (
            1.0 - null_values
        )

    else:

        observed_drift = observed
        null_drift = null_values

    p_value = (
        np.sum(
            null_drift
            >= observed_drift
        )
        + 1
    ) / (
        n_permutations
        + 1
    )

    return (
        float(observed),
        float(p_value),
    )


# ============================================================
# One comparison
# ============================================================


def evaluate_comparison(
    X_a: np.ndarray,
    X_b: np.ndarray,
    window_a: int,
    window_b: int,
    condition: str,
    n_bootstrap: int,
    n_permutations: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> List[Dict]:

    metrics = [
        "js_divergence",
        "cosine_distance",
        "l1_distance",
        "l2_distance",
        "spearman_rank_correlation",
    ]

    rows = []

    for metric in metrics:

        (
            observed,
            ci_lower,
            ci_upper,
            bootstrap_bias,
            percentile_position,
        ) = bootstrap_drift(
            X_a=X_a,
            X_b=X_b,
            metric=metric,
            n_bootstrap=n_bootstrap,
            rng=rng,
        )

        _, p_value = permutation_test(
            X_a=X_a,
            X_b=X_b,
            metric=metric,
            n_permutations=n_permutations,
            rng=rng,
        )

        rows.append(
            {
                "comparison":
                    f"w{window_a}_w{window_b}",

                "window_a":
                    window_a,

                "window_b":
                    window_b,

                "condition":
                    condition,

                "metric":
                    metric,

                "observed_value":
                    observed,

                "bootstrap_ci_lower":
                    ci_lower,

                "bootstrap_ci_upper":
                    ci_upper,

                "bootstrap_mean":
                    np.nan
                    if np.isnan(bootstrap_bias)
                    else observed + bootstrap_bias,

                "bootstrap_bias":
                    bootstrap_bias,

                "bootstrap_percentile_position":
                    percentile_position,

                "permutation_pvalue":
                    p_value,

                "n_observations_a":
                    len(X_a),

                "n_observations_b":
                    len(X_b),

                "n_features":
                    X_a.shape[1],

                "n_bootstrap":
                    n_bootstrap,

                "n_permutations":
                    n_permutations,
            }
        )

    return rows


# ============================================================
# Top-K significance
# ============================================================


def evaluate_top_k(
    X_a: np.ndarray,
    X_b: np.ndarray,
    window_a: int,
    window_b: int,
    n_permutations: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> List[Dict]:

    p = mean_explanation_distribution(
        X_a
    )

    q = mean_explanation_distribution(
        X_b
    )

    rows = []

    combined = np.vstack(
        [
            X_a,
            X_b,
        ]
    )

    n_a = len(X_a)

    for k in TOP_K_VALUES:

        observed = jaccard_similarity(
            top_k_features(p, k),
            top_k_features(q, k),
        )

        null_values = np.empty(
            n_permutations,
            dtype=float,
        )

        for i in range(
            n_permutations
        ):

            indices = rng.permutation(
                len(combined)
            )

            perm_a = combined[
                indices[:n_a]
            ]

            perm_b = combined[
                indices[n_a:]
            ]

            p_perm = (
                mean_explanation_distribution(
                    perm_a
                )
            )

            q_perm = (
                mean_explanation_distribution(
                    perm_b
                )
            )

            null_values[i] = (
                jaccard_similarity(
                    top_k_features(
                        p_perm,
                        k,
                    ),
                    top_k_features(
                        q_perm,
                        k,
                    ),
                )
            )

        observed_instability = (
            1.0 - observed
        )

        null_instability = (
            1.0 - null_values
        )

        p_value = (
            np.sum(
                null_instability
                >= observed_instability
            )
            + 1
        ) / (
            n_permutations
            + 1
        )

        rows.append(
            {
                "comparison":
                    f"w{window_a}_w{window_b}",

                "window_a":
                    window_a,

                "window_b":
                    window_b,

                "top_k":
                    k,

                "observed_jaccard":
                    observed,

                "observed_instability":
                    observed_instability,

                "permutation_pvalue":
                    float(p_value),

                "n_observations_a":
                    len(X_a),

                "n_observations_b":
                    len(X_b),

                "n_features":
                    X_a.shape[1],

                "n_permutations":
                    n_permutations,
            }
        )

    return rows


# ============================================================
# Class-specific evaluation
# ============================================================


def evaluate_class_comparisons(
    windows: Dict[int, pd.DataFrame],
    matrices: Dict[int, np.ndarray],
    n_bootstrap: int,
    n_permutations: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> List[Dict]:

    rows = []

    comparisons = [
        (1, 2),
        (2, 3),
        (3, 4),
    ]

    for window_a, window_b in comparisons:

        for fraud_value, class_name in [
            (1, "fraud"),
            (0, "nonfraud"),
        ]:

            df_a = windows[
                window_a
            ]

            df_b = windows[
                window_b
            ]

            mask_a = (
                df_a["isFraud"]
                .to_numpy()
                == fraud_value
            )

            mask_b = (
                df_b["isFraud"]
                .to_numpy()
                == fraud_value
            )

            X_a = matrices[
                window_a
            ][mask_a]

            X_b = matrices[
                window_b
            ][mask_b]

            if (
                len(X_a) == 0
                or len(X_b) == 0
            ):
                continue

            class_rows = evaluate_comparison(
                X_a=X_a,
                X_b=X_b,
                window_a=window_a,
                window_b=window_b,
                condition=class_name,
                n_bootstrap=n_bootstrap,
                n_permutations=n_permutations,
                rng=rng,
            )

            rows.extend(
                class_rows
            )

    return rows


# ============================================================
# Multiple comparison correction
# ============================================================


def apply_fdr(
    df: pd.DataFrame,
    pvalue_column: str = "permutation_pvalue",
    group_columns: List[str] | None = None,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    df = df.copy()

    df[
        "fdr_adjusted_pvalue"
    ] = np.nan

    df[
        "significant_fdr"
    ] = False

    if df.empty:
        return df

    if group_columns is None:

        group_columns = []

    if group_columns:

        grouped = df.groupby(
            group_columns,
            sort=False,
        )

        for _, group in grouped:

            indices = group.index

            adjusted = (
                false_discovery_control(
                    group[
                        pvalue_column
                    ].to_numpy(),
                    method="bh",
                )
            )

            df.loc[
                indices,
                "fdr_adjusted_pvalue",
            ] = adjusted

    else:

        adjusted = (
            false_discovery_control(
                df[
                    pvalue_column
                ].to_numpy(),
                method="bh",
            )
        )

        df[
            "fdr_adjusted_pvalue"
        ] = adjusted

    df[
        "significant_fdr"
    ] = (
        df[
            "fdr_adjusted_pvalue"
        ]
        < ALPHA
    )

    return df


# ============================================================
# Main experiment
# ============================================================


def run_experiment(
    input_dir: Path = DEFAULT_INPUT_DIR,
    output_dir: Path = DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR,
    n_bootstrap: int = DEFAULT_N_BOOTSTRAP,
    n_permutations: int = DEFAULT_N_PERMUTATIONS,
    random_state: int = DEFAULT_RANDOM_STATE,
):

    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "BASELINE 1 — TEMPORAL EXIFFI DRIFT "
        "STATISTICAL VALIDATION"
    )
    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        f"\nBootstrap samples: {n_bootstrap}"
    )

    print(
        f"Permutation samples: "
        f"{n_permutations}"
    )

    print(
        f"Random state: {random_state}"
    )

    print(
        f"FDR alpha: {ALPHA}"
    )

    rng = np.random.default_rng(
        random_state
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Load
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[1/7] Loading temporal explanations..."
    )

    windows = {}
    matrices = {}
    feature_names = {}

    for window in WINDOWS:

        df = load_window(
            window,
            input_dir,
        )

        X, names = (
            extract_importance_matrix(
                df
            )
        )

        windows[window] = df
        matrices[window] = X
        feature_names[window] = names

    reference_features = (
        feature_names[1]
    )

    for window in WINDOWS[1:]:

        if (
            feature_names[window]
            != reference_features
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Feature sets differ across "
                f"windows 1 and {window}."
            )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Overall drift
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[2/7] Evaluating overall temporal drift..."
    )

    overall_rows = []

    for window_a, window_b in [
        (1, 2),
        (2, 3),
        (3, 4),
    ]:

        print(
            f"\n  Window {window_a} → "
            f"Window {window_b}"
        )

        rows = evaluate_comparison(
            X_a=matrices[window_a],
            X_b=matrices[window_b],
            window_a=window_a,
            window_b=window_b,
            condition="overall",
            n_bootstrap=n_bootstrap,
            n_permutations=n_permutations,
            rng=rng,
        )

        overall_rows.extend(
            rows
        )

        for row in rows:

            print(
                f"    {row['metric']}: "
                f"{row['observed_value']:.6f} "
                f"["
                f"{row['bootstrap_ci_lower']:.6f}, "
                f"{row['bootstrap_ci_upper']:.6f}"
                f"] "
                f"p="
                f"{row['permutation_pvalue']:.6g}"
            )

    overall_df = pd.DataFrame(
        overall_rows
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Top-K
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[3/7] Evaluating Top-K stability..."
    )

    top_k_rows = []

    for window_a, window_b in [
        (1, 2),
        (2, 3),
        (3, 4),
    ]:

        rows = evaluate_top_k(
            X_a=matrices[window_a],
            X_b=matrices[window_b],
            window_a=window_a,
            window_b=window_b,
            n_permutations=n_permutations,
            rng=rng,
        )

        top_k_rows.extend(
            rows
        )

    top_k_df = pd.DataFrame(
        top_k_rows
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Class-specific
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[4/7] Evaluating class-specific drift..."
    )

    class_rows = evaluate_class_comparisons(
        windows=windows,
        matrices=matrices,
        n_bootstrap=n_bootstrap,
        n_permutations=n_permutations,
        rng=rng,
    )

    class_df = pd.DataFrame(
        class_rows
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Multiple-comparison correction
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[5/7] Applying Benjamini-Hochberg FDR correction..."
    )

    # Overall metrics:
    # correct across all overall metric/window tests.
    overall_df = apply_fdr(
        overall_df,
        group_columns=None,
    )

    # Top-K:
    # correct across all Top-K/window tests.
    top_k_df = apply_fdr(
        top_k_df,
        group_columns=None,
    )

    # Class-specific:
    # correct across all class/metric/window tests.
    class_df = apply_fdr(
        class_df,
        group_columns=None,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Save
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[6/7] Saving statistical results..."
    )

    output_dir.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    overall_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_drift_significance.csv"
    )

    top_k_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_top_k_significance.csv"
    )

    class_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_class_drift_significance.csv"
    )

    diagnostics_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_bootstrap_diagnostics.csv"
    )

    overall_df.to_csv(
        overall_path,
        index=False,
    )

    top_k_df.to_csv(
        top_k_path,
        index=False,
    )

    class_df.to_csv(
        class_path,
        index=False,
    )

    diagnostics_columns = [
        "comparison",
        "condition",
        "metric",
        "observed_value",
        "bootstrap_mean",
        "bootstrap_bias",
        "bootstrap_percentile_position",
        "bootstrap_ci_lower",
        "bootstrap_ci_upper",
    ]

    overall_df[
        diagnostics_columns
    ].to_csv(
        diagnostics_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"Overall results:\n{overall_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"Top-K results:\n{top_k_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"Class-specific results:\n{class_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"Bootstrap diagnostics:\n"
        f"{diagnostics_path}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Summary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[7/7] Statistical validation summary"
    )

    print(
        "\nOverall drift:"
    )

    print(
        overall_df[
            [
                "comparison",
                "metric",
                "observed_value",
                "bootstrap_ci_lower",
                "bootstrap_ci_upper",
                "permutation_pvalue",
                "fdr_adjusted_pvalue",
                "significant_fdr",
            ]
        ].to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nTop-K stability:"
    )

    print(
        top_k_df[
            [
                "comparison",
                "top_k",
                "observed_jaccard",
                "observed_instability",
                "permutation_pvalue",
                "fdr_adjusted_pvalue",
                "significant_fdr",
            ]
        ].to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nClass-specific drift:"
    )

    print(
        class_df[
            [
                "comparison",
                "condition",
                "metric",
                "observed_value",
                "permutation_pvalue",
                "fdr_adjusted_pvalue",
                "significant_fdr",
            ]
        ].to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\n"
        + "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "TEMPORAL EXIFFI DRIFT STATISTICAL "
        "VALIDATION COMPLETE"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 70
    )

    return (
        overall_df,
        top_k_df,
        class_df,
    )


# ============================================================
# CLI
# ============================================================


def main():

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Statistically validate temporal "
            "ExIFFI explanation drift using "
            "bootstrap confidence intervals, "
            "permutation testing, and "
            "Benjamini-Hochberg FDR correction."
        )
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--input-dir",
        default=str(
            DEFAULT_INPUT_DIR
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir",
        default=str(
            DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--bootstrap",
        type=int,
        default=DEFAULT_N_BOOTSTRAP,
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--permutations",
        type=int,
        default=DEFAULT_N_PERMUTATIONS,
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--random-state",
        type=int,
        default=DEFAULT_RANDOM_STATE,
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    run_experiment(
        input_dir=Path(
            args.input_dir
        ),
        output_dir=Path(
            args.output_dir
        ),
        n_bootstrap=args.bootstrap,
        n_permutations=args.permutations,
        random_state=args.random_state,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
=== FILE: ./scripts/evaluate_exiffi_fidelity.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from efd.data.preprocessing import (
    FeaturePolicy,
    NumericTransactionPreprocessor,
)

from efd.data.temporal import (
    chronological_windows,
)

from efd.models.isolation_forest import (
    IsolationForestDetector,
)

from efd.explanations.exiffi import (
    ExIFFI,
)

from efd.evaluation.explanation_fidelity import (
    evaluate_fidelity_matrix,
)


def run_experiment(
    transactions_path: str,
    train_fraction: float = 0.60,
    n_future_windows: int = 1,
    n_estimators: int = 300,
    random_state: int = 42,
    reference_date: str = "2017-12-01",
    sample_size: int = 100,
):

    print("=" * 70)
    print("BASELINE 1 — EXIFFI LOCAL FIDELITY")
    print("=" * 70)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Load data
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print("\n[1/7] Loading transaction dataset...")

    df = pd.read_csv(
        transactions_path,
        low_memory=False,
    )

    print(
        f"Loaded {len(df):,} transactions."
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Chronological split
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[2/7] Creating chronological split..."
    )

    train, future_windows = chronological_windows(
        df,
        time_col="TransactionDT",
        train_fraction=train_fraction,
        n_future_windows=n_future_windows,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Preprocessing
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[3/7] Fitting preprocessing..."
    )

    preprocessor = NumericTransactionPreprocessor(
        policy=FeaturePolicy(
            target="isFraud",
            temporal="TransactionDT",
            identifier="TransactionID",
            max_missing_rate=0.95,
        )
    )

    x_train = (
        preprocessor
        .fit_transform(train)
    )

    feature_names = (
        preprocessor.feature_names_
    )

    print(
        f"Features: {len(feature_names)}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Train Isolation Forest
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[4/7] Training Isolation Forest..."
    )

    detector = IsolationForestDetector(
        n_estimators=n_estimators,
        max_samples="auto",
        contamination="auto",
        random_state=random_state,
        n_jobs=-1,
    )

    detector.fit(
        x_train
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Build ExIFFI explanations
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[5/7] Generating ExIFFI explanations..."
    )

    explain = ExIFFI(
        model=detector.model,
        feature_names=feature_names,
    )

    # Use first future window.
    window = future_windows[0]

    X_window = (
        preprocessor
        .transform(window)
    )

    n = min(
        sample_size,
        len(X_window),
    )

    X_sample = X_window.iloc[:n].to_numpy(dtype=float)

    explanations = [
        explain.explain(
            X_sample[i],
            sample_index=i,
        )
        for i in range(n)
    ]

    print(
        f"Generated explanations for {n} observations."
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Define baseline
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[6/7] Evaluating feature perturbation fidelity..."
    )

    # Median feature values from training data.
    #
    # IMPORTANT:
    # baseline is calculated from TRAIN ONLY.
    baseline = np.median(
        x_train,
        axis=0,
    )

    k_values = [
        1,
        3,
        5,
        10,
        20,
    ]

    results = evaluate_fidelity_matrix(
        model=detector.model,
        X=X_sample,
        explanations=explanations,
        baseline=baseline,
        k_values=k_values,
    )

    result_rows = [
        {
            "sample_index": r.sample_index,
            "baseline_score": r.baseline_score,
            "top_k": r.top_k,
            "top_k_mass": r.top_k_mass,
            "perturbed_score": r.perturbed_score,
            "score_change": r.score_change,
            "relative_score_change": (
                r.relative_score_change
            ),
        }
        for r in results
    ]

    result_df = pd.DataFrame(
        result_rows
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Save
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[7/7] Saving fidelity results..."
    )

    output_dir = Path(
        "results/tables"
    )

    output_dir.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    output_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_w1_fidelity.csv"
    )

    result_df.to_csv(
        output_path,
        index=False,
    )

    summary = (
        result_df
        .groupby("top_k")
        .agg(
            n_observations=(
                "sample_index",
                "count",
            ),
            mean_top_k_mass=(
                "top_k_mass",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_top_k_mass=(
                "top_k_mass",
                "median",
            ),
            mean_score_change=(
                "score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_score_change=(
                "score_change",
                "median",
            ),
            mean_relative_score_change=(
                "relative_score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_relative_score_change=(
                "relative_score_change",
                "median",
            ),
        )
        .reset_index()
    )

    summary_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_w1_fidelity_summary.csv"
    )

    summary.to_csv(
        summary_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"\nResults saved to:\n{output_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"\nSummary saved to:\n{summary_path}"
    )

    print(
        "\nFidelity summary:"
    )

    print(
        summary.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    return result_df, summary


def main():

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--transactions",
        required=True,
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--sample-size",
        type=int,
        default=100,
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    run_experiment(
        transactions_path=args.transactions,
        sample_size=args.sample_size,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
=== FILE: ./scripts/generate_exiffi_temporal_explanations.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from efd.data.preprocessing import (
    FeaturePolicy,
    NumericTransactionPreprocessor,
)

from efd.data.temporal import (
    chronological_windows,
)

from efd.models.isolation_forest import (
    IsolationForestDetector,
)

from efd.explanations.exiffi import (
    ExIFFI,
)


def stratified_sample_indices(
    y: pd.Series,
    sample_size: int,
    random_state: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Generate stratified sample indices.

    The current experiment uses binary fraud labels and attempts
    to sample equally from the two classes.

    If one class contains fewer observations than the requested
    allocation, the remaining slots are filled from the other
    class.

    Parameters
    ----------
    y:
        Fraud labels aligned to the window observations.

    sample_size:
        Number of observations to sample.

    random_state:
        Random seed.

    Returns
    -------
    np.ndarray
        Positional indices into the original window.
    """

    if sample_size <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            "sample_size must be greater than zero."
        )

    if sample_size > len(y):
        raise ValueError(
            "sample_size cannot exceed the number "
            "of observations in the window."
        )

    rng = np.random.default_rng(
        random_state
    )

    y_values = y.to_numpy()

    classes = np.unique(y_values)

    if len(classes) < 2:
        raise ValueError(
            "Stratified sampling requires at least "
            "two classes in the window."
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Binary stratified sampling.
    #
    # We intentionally allocate approximately half the sample
    # to each class.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    n_classes = len(classes)

    base_per_class = sample_size // n_classes
    remainder = sample_size % n_classes

    selected_indices: list[int] = []

    for class_position, class_value in enumerate(classes):

        class_indices = np.flatnonzero(
            y_values == class_value
        )

        target_n = base_per_class

        if class_position < remainder:
            target_n += 1

        if target_n > len(class_indices):
            target_n = len(class_indices)

        selected = rng.choice(
            class_indices,
            size=target_n,
            replace=False,
        )
