        selected_indices.extend(
            selected.tolist()
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # If one class did not have enough observations, fill the
    # remaining slots from observations not already selected.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    selected_set = set(
        selected_indices
    )

    remaining_n = (
        sample_size
        - len(selected_indices)
    )

    if remaining_n > 0:

        available = np.array(
            [
                i
                for i in range(len(y_values))
                if i not in selected_set
            ],
            dtype=int,
        )

        if remaining_n > len(available):
            raise ValueError(
                "Unable to construct requested "
                "stratified sample."
            )

        additional = rng.choice(
            available,
            size=remaining_n,
            replace=False,
        )

        selected_indices.extend(
            additional.tolist()
        )

    # Shuffle final sample so that the resulting observations
    # are not grouped by class.
    selected_indices = np.asarray(
        selected_indices,
        dtype=int,
    )

    rng.shuffle(
        selected_indices
    )

    return selected_indices


def random_sample_indices(
    n_observations: int,
    sample_size: int,
    random_state: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Generate a random sample of positional indices.
    """

    if sample_size <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            "sample_size must be greater than zero."
        )

    if sample_size > n_observations:
        raise ValueError(
            "sample_size cannot exceed the number "
            "of observations in the window."
        )

    rng = np.random.default_rng(
        random_state
    )

    return rng.choice(
        n_observations,
        size=sample_size,
        replace=False,
    )


def sequential_sample_indices(
    n_observations: int,
    sample_size: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Generate a sequential sample from the beginning
    of the window.
    """

    if sample_size <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            "sample_size must be greater than zero."
        )

    if sample_size > n_observations:
        raise ValueError(
            "sample_size cannot exceed the number "
            "of observations in the window."
        )

    return np.arange(
        sample_size,
        dtype=int,
    )


def select_sample_indices(
    window: pd.DataFrame,
    sample_size: int,
    strategy: str,
    random_state: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Select sample indices according to the requested strategy.
    """

    if strategy == "sequential":

        return sequential_sample_indices(
            n_observations=len(window),
            sample_size=sample_size,
        )

    if strategy == "random":

        return random_sample_indices(
            n_observations=len(window),
            sample_size=sample_size,
            random_state=random_state,
        )

    if strategy == "stratified":

        if "isFraud" not in window.columns:
            raise ValueError(
                "Stratified sampling requires "
                "'isFraud' in the window."
            )

        return stratified_sample_indices(
            y=window["isFraud"],
            sample_size=sample_size,
            random_state=random_state,
        )

    raise ValueError(
        f"Unknown sampling strategy: {strategy}"
    )


def build_explanation_dataframe(
    explanations,
    X_sample: np.ndarray,
    y_sample: np.ndarray,
    feature_names: list[str],
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Convert ExIFFI explanations into the wide-format CSV
    used by the downstream explanation evaluation pipeline.

    The resulting dataframe contains:

        sample_index
        isFraud
        anomaly_score
        top_1_feature
        top_1_importance
        ...
        importance__<feature>

    The full normalized ExIFFI importance vector is retained
    for every observation.
    """

    rows = []

    for explanation, fraud_label in zip(
        explanations,
        y_sample,
    ):

        normalized = explanation.normalized

        row = {
            "sample_index": explanation.sample_index,
            "isFraud": int(fraud_label),
            "anomaly_score": float(
                -explanation.anomaly_score
            ),
        }

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Full importance vector.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        for feature_name, importance in zip(
            feature_names,
            normalized,
        ):

            row[
                f"importance__{feature_name}"
            ] = float(importance)

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Top feature information.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        top_features = explanation.top_features(
            feature_names=feature_names,
            k=10,
        )

        for rank in range(1, 11):

            if rank <= len(top_features):

                feature, importance = (
                    top_features[rank - 1]
                )

                row[
                    f"top_{rank}_feature"
                ] = feature

                row[
                    f"top_{rank}_importance"
                ] = float(importance)

            else:

                row[
                    f"top_{rank}_feature"
                ] = None

                row[
                    f"top_{rank}_importance"
                ] = 0.0

        rows.append(row)

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def run_experiment(
    transactions_path: str,
    train_fraction: float = 0.60,
    n_future_windows: int = 4,
    n_estimators: int = 300,
    random_state: int = 42,
    reference_date: str = "2017-12-01",
    sample_size: int = 100,
    sample_strategy: str = "sequential",
):
    """
    Generate ExIFFI explanations across chronological
    future windows using a static Isolation Forest.

    The model and preprocessing are fitted ONLY on the
    training period.

    Explanations are then generated independently for
    each future window.

    Parameters
    ----------
    transactions_path:
        Path to IEEE-CIS train_transaction.csv.

    train_fraction:
        Fraction of observations used for training.

    n_future_windows:
        Number of future chronological windows.

    n_estimators:
        Number of Isolation Forest trees.

    random_state:
        Global random seed.

    reference_date:
        Reference date used by temporal utilities if required.

    sample_size:
        Number of observations sampled from each future window.

    sample_strategy:
        One of:

            sequential
            random
            stratified
    """

    print("=" * 70)
    print("BASELINE 1 — TEMPORAL EXIFFI EXPLANATIONS")
    print("=" * 70)

    print()
    print(
        f"Sampling strategy: {sample_strategy}"
    )

    print(
        f"Sample size per window: {sample_size}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Load data
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[1/7] Loading transaction dataset..."
    )

    df = pd.read_csv(
        transactions_path,
        low_memory=False,
    )

    print(
        f"Loaded {len(df):,} transactions "
        f"and {len(df.columns)} columns."
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Chronological split
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[2/7] Creating chronological windows..."
    )

    train, future_windows = chronological_windows(
        df,
        time_col="TransactionDT",
        train_fraction=train_fraction,
        n_future_windows=n_future_windows,
    )

    print(
        f"Training observations: "
        f"{len(train):,}"
    )

    print(
        f"Future windows: "
        f"{len(future_windows)}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Preprocessing
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[3/7] Fitting preprocessing "
        "on training period only..."
    )

    preprocessor = NumericTransactionPreprocessor(
        policy=FeaturePolicy(
            target="isFraud",
            temporal="TransactionDT",
            identifier="TransactionID",
            max_missing_rate=0.95,
        )
    )

    x_train = (
        preprocessor
        .fit_transform(train)
    )

    feature_names = (
        preprocessor.feature_names_
    )

    print(
        f"Selected features: "
        f"{len(feature_names)}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Train static Isolation Forest
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[4/7] Training static Isolation Forest..."
    )

    detector = IsolationForestDetector(
        n_estimators=n_estimators,
        max_samples="auto",
        contamination="auto",
        random_state=random_state,
        n_jobs=-1,
    )

    detector.fit(
        x_train
    )

    print(
        f"Isolation Forest trained with "
        f"{n_estimators} trees."
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Initialize ExIFFI
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[5/7] Initializing ExIFFI..."
    )

    explain = ExIFFI(
        model=detector.model,
        feature_names=feature_names,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Generate temporal explanations
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[6/7] Generating temporal explanations..."
    )

    output_dir = Path(
        "results/explanations"
    )

    output_dir.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    metadata_rows = []

    for window_number, window in enumerate(
        future_windows,
        start=1,
    ):

        print()
        print(
            f"Window {window_number}"
        )

        print(
            f"  Observations: "
            f"{len(window):,}"
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Date information.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        if "TransactionDT" in window.columns:

            transaction_dates = pd.to_datetime(
                window["TransactionDT"],
                unit="s",
                origin=reference_date,
                errors="coerce",
            )

            date_start = transaction_dates.min()

            date_end = transaction_dates.max()

            print(
                f"  Date range: "
                f"{date_start.date() if pd.notna(date_start) else 'N/A'} "
                f"→ "
                f"{date_end.date() if pd.notna(date_end) else 'N/A'}"
            )

        else:

            date_start = None
            date_end = None

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Fraud statistics.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        if "isFraud" in window.columns:

            fraud_count = int(
                window["isFraud"]
                .sum()
            )

            nonfraud_count = (
                len(window)
                - fraud_count
            )

            print(
                f"  Fraud observations: "
                f"{fraud_count:,}"
            )

            print(
                f"  Non-fraud observations: "
                f"{nonfraud_count:,}"
            )

        else:

            fraud_count = None
            nonfraud_count = None

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Transform future window using the SAME preprocessor
        # fitted on training data.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        X_window = (
            preprocessor
            .transform(window)
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Select sample indices.
        #
        # IMPORTANT:
        # These indices refer to the positional rows of the
        # original future window and therefore remain aligned
        # with the fraud labels.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        sample_indices = select_sample_indices(
            window=window,
            sample_size=min(
                sample_size,
                len(window),
            ),
            strategy=sample_strategy,
            random_state=(
                random_state
                + window_number
            ),
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Extract sampled feature matrix.
        #
        # This is the critical fix for the stratified sampling
        # bug. If X_window is a DataFrame, convert it to a
        # NumPy array before using positional indexing.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        if isinstance(
            X_window,
            pd.DataFrame,
        ):

            X_sample = (
                X_window
                .iloc[sample_indices]
                .to_numpy(
                    dtype=float
                )
            )

        else:

            X_window_array = np.asarray(
                X_window,
                dtype=float,
            )

            X_sample = (
                X_window_array[
                    sample_indices
                ]
            )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Extract labels using THE SAME positional indices.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        y_sample = (
            window["isFraud"]
            .iloc[sample_indices]
            .to_numpy(
                dtype=int
            )
        )

        n = len(X_sample)

        print(
            f"  Sampled observations: "
            f"{n}"
        )

        if sample_strategy == "stratified":

            sampled_fraud = int(
                y_sample.sum()
            )

            sampled_nonfraud = (
                n
                - sampled_fraud
            )

            print(
                f"  Sampled fraud: "
                f"{sampled_fraud}"
            )

            print(
                f"  Sampled non-fraud: "
                f"{sampled_nonfraud}"
            )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Sanity checks.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        if X_sample.ndim != 2:

            raise ValueError(
                "X_sample must be a "
                "two-dimensional matrix."
            )

        if X_sample.shape[1] != len(
            feature_names
        ):

            raise ValueError(
                "Sample feature count does not "
                "match ExIFFI feature count."
            )

        if len(X_sample) != len(
            y_sample
        ):

            raise ValueError(
                "Sampled feature matrix and "
                "labels are not aligned."
            )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Generate explanations.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        explanations = []

        for i in range(n):

            explanation = explain.explain(
                X_sample[i],
                sample_index=sample_indices[i],
            )

            explanations.append(
                explanation
            )

        print(
            f"  Generated explanations: "
            f"{len(explanations)}"
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Build output dataframe.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        explanation_df = (
            build_explanation_dataframe(
                explanations=explanations,
                X_sample=X_sample,
                y_sample=y_sample,
                feature_names=feature_names,
            )
        )

        output_path = (
            output_dir
            / (
                "static_if_exiffi_"
                f"w{window_number}_sample.csv"
            )
        )

        explanation_df.to_csv(
            output_path,
            index=False,
        )

        print(
            f"  Saved: {output_path}"
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Metadata.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        metadata_rows.append(
            {
                "window": window_number,
                "n_observations": len(window),
                "n_sampled": n,
                "n_fraud": fraud_count,
                "n_nonfraud": nonfraud_count,
                "sampled_fraud": int(
                    y_sample.sum()
                ),
                "sampled_nonfraud": int(
                    len(y_sample)
                    - y_sample.sum()
                ),
                "sample_strategy": sample_strategy,
                "date_start": (
                    date_start
                    if date_start is not None
                    else ""
                ),
                "date_end": (
                    date_end
                    if date_end is not None
                    else ""
                ),
                "n_features": len(
                    feature_names
                ),
                "n_estimators": n_estimators,
                "random_state": random_state,
            }
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Save experiment metadata
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[7/7] Saving temporal experiment metadata..."
    )

    metadata_df = pd.DataFrame(
        metadata_rows
    )

    metadata_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_metadata.csv"
    )

    metadata_df.to_csv(
        metadata_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"\nMetadata saved to:"
        f"\n{metadata_path}"
    )

    # Also preserve a human-readable metadata file.
    txt_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_metadata.txt"
    )

    with txt_path.open(
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8",
    ) as f:

        f.write(
            "BASELINE 1 — TEMPORAL EXIFFI "
            "EXPLANATIONS\n"
        )

        f.write(
            "=" * 70
            + "\n\n"
        )

        f.write(
            f"Transactions: "
            f"{transactions_path}\n"
        )

        f.write(
            f"Training fraction: "
            f"{train_fraction}\n"
        )

        f.write(
            f"Future windows: "
            f"{n_future_windows}\n"
        )

        f.write(
            f"Sample size per window: "
            f"{sample_size}\n"
        )

        f.write(
            f"Sampling strategy: "
            f"{sample_strategy}\n"
        )

        f.write(
            f"Random state: "
            f"{random_state}\n"
        )

        f.write(
            f"Isolation Forest trees: "
            f"{n_estimators}\n"
        )

        f.write(
            f"Features: "
            f"{len(feature_names)}\n\n"
        )

        f.write(
            metadata_df.to_string(
                index=False
            )
        )

        f.write("\n")

    print(
        f"Human-readable metadata saved to:"
        f"\n{txt_path}"
    )

    print(
        "\nTemporal ExIFFI generation complete."
    )

    return metadata_df


def main():

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Generate temporal ExIFFI explanations "
            "for future chronological windows."
        )
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--transactions",
        required=True,
        help=(
            "Path to "
            "train_transaction.csv"
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--train-fraction",
        type=float,
        default=0.60,
        help=(
            "Fraction of observations used "
            "for training. Default: 0.60"
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--future-windows",
        type=int,
        default=4,
        help=(
            "Number of future windows. "
            "Default: 4"
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--n-estimators",
        type=int,
        default=300,
        help=(
            "Number of Isolation Forest trees. "
            "Default: 300"
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--sample-size",
        type=int,
        default=100,
        help=(
            "Number of observations sampled "
            "from each future window. "
            "Default: 100"
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--sample-strategy",
        choices=[
            "sequential",
            "random",
            "stratified",
        ],
        default="sequential",
        help=(
            "Sampling strategy. "
            "Default: sequential"
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--random-state",
        type=int,
        default=42,
        help=(
            "Random seed. "
            "Default: 42"
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--reference-date",
        default="2017-12-01",
        help=(
            "Reference date for TransactionDT. "
            "Default: 2017-12-01"
        ),
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    run_experiment(
        transactions_path=args.transactions,
        train_fraction=args.train_fraction,
        n_future_windows=args.future_windows,
        n_estimators=args.n_estimators,
        random_state=args.random_state,
        reference_date=args.reference_date,
        sample_size=args.sample_size,
        sample_strategy=args.sample_strategy,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
=== FILE: ./scripts/analyze_exiffi_temporal_drift.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from efd.evaluation.explanation_drift import (
    explanation_drift_summary,
    feature_drift_table,
    rank_correlation,
    top_k_jaccard,
)


INPUT_DIR = Path(
    "results/explanations"
)

OUTPUT_DIR = Path(
    "results/tables"
)

WINDOWS = [
    1,
    2,
    3,
    4,
]

TOP_K_VALUES = [
    1,
    5,
    10,
    20,
]


def load_window(
    window: int,
):

    path = (
        INPUT_DIR
        / f"static_if_exiffi_w{window}_sample.csv"
    )

    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Missing explanation file: {path}"
        )

    df = pd.read_csv(
        path
    )

    importance_columns = [
        column
        for column in df.columns
        if column.startswith(
            "importance__"
        )
    ]

    if not importance_columns:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No importance columns found in {path}"
        )

    importance = (
        df[importance_columns]
        .to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )
    )

    feature_names = [
        column.replace(
            "importance__",
            "",
            1,
        )
        for column in importance_columns
    ]

    return (
        df,
        importance,
        feature_names,
    )


def main():

    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "BASELINE 1 — "
        "TEMPORAL EXIFFI EXPLANATION DRIFT"
    )
    print("=" * 70)

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    print(
        "\n[1/5] Loading temporal explanations..."
    )

    windows = {}

    for window in WINDOWS:

        (
            df,
            importance,
            feature_names,
        ) = load_window(
            window
        )

        windows[window] = (
            df,
            importance,
            feature_names,
        )

        print(
            f"Window {window}: "
            f"{len(df):,} observations, "
            f"{importance.shape[1]:,} features"
        )

    print(
        "\n[2/5] Calculating window-to-window drift..."
    )

    summaries = []

    feature_tables = []

    stability_rows = []

    class_rows = []

    for window_a, window_b in zip(
        WINDOWS[:-1],
        WINDOWS[1:],
    ):

        (
            df_a,
            X_a,
            features_a,
        ) = windows[window_a]

        (
            df_b,
            X_b,
            features_b,
        ) = windows[window_b]

        comparison = (
            f"w{window_a}_w{window_b}"
        )

        print(
            f"\n  {comparison}"
        )

        if features_a != features_b:
            raise ValueError(
                "Feature schema mismatch "
                f"between W{window_a} and W{window_b}."
            )

        summary = (
            explanation_drift_summary(
                X_a,
                X_b,
                f"w{window_a}",
                f"w{window_b}",
            )
        )

        summary[
            "window_a"
        ] = window_a

        summary[
            "window_b"
        ] = window_b

        summary[
            "n_features"
        ] = X_a.shape[1]

        summary[
            "n_observations_a"
        ] = len(X_a)

        summary[
            "n_observations_b"
        ] = len(X_b)

        summaries.append(
            summary
        )

        feature_table = (
            feature_drift_table(
                X_a,
                X_b,
                features_a,
                f"w{window_a}",
                f"w{window_b}",
            )
        )

        feature_table.insert(
            0,
            "comparison",
            comparison,
        )

        feature_tables.append(
            feature_table
        )

        print(
            f"    JS divergence: "
            f"{summary['js_divergence']:.6f}"
        )

        print(
            f"    Cosine distance: "
            f"{summary['cosine_distance']:.6f}"
        )

        print(
            f"    Spearman correlation: "
            f"{summary['spearman_rank_correlation']:.6f}"
        )

        for k in TOP_K_VALUES:

            stability_rows.append(
                {
                    "comparison":
                        comparison,

                    "window_a":
                        window_a,

                    "window_b":
                        window_b,

                    "top_k":
                        k,

                    "jaccard":
                        top_k_jaccard(
                            X_a,
                            X_b,
                            k,
                        ),

                    "spearman_rank_correlation":
                        rank_correlation(
                            X_a,
                            X_b,
                        ),
                }
            )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Class-specific drift
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        for fraud_value, class_name in [
            (1, "fraud"),
            (0, "nonfraud"),
        ]:

            if (
                "isFraud" not in df_a.columns
                or "isFraud" not in df_b.columns
            ):
                continue

            mask_a = (
                df_a["isFraud"]
                .to_numpy()
                == fraud_value
            )

            mask_b = (
                df_b["isFraud"]
                .to_numpy()
                == fraud_value
            )

            if not mask_a.any():
                continue

            if not mask_b.any():
                continue

            class_summary = (
                explanation_drift_summary(
                    X_a[mask_a],
                    X_b[mask_b],
                    f"w{window_a}_{class_name}",
                    f"w{window_b}_{class_name}",
                )
            )

            class_summary[
                "window_a"
            ] = window_a

            class_summary[
                "window_b"
            ] = window_b

            class_summary[
                "class"
            ] = class_name

            class_summary[
                "n_observations_a"
            ] = int(mask_a.sum())

            class_summary[
                "n_observations_b"
            ] = int(mask_b.sum())

            class_rows.append(
                class_summary
            )

    print(
        "\n[3/5] Saving overall drift results..."
    )

    summary_df = pd.DataFrame(
        summaries
    )

    summary_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_drift_summary.csv"
    )

    summary_df.to_csv(
        summary_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"Saved:\n{summary_path}"
    )

    print(
        "\n[4/5] Saving feature and Top-K drift..."
    )

    feature_drift_df = pd.concat(
        feature_tables,
        ignore_index=True,
    )

    feature_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_feature_drift.csv"
    )

    feature_drift_df.to_csv(
        feature_path,
        index=False,
    )

    stability_df = pd.DataFrame(
        stability_rows
    )

    stability_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_top_k_stability.csv"
    )

    stability_df.to_csv(
        stability_path,
        index=False,
    )

    class_df = pd.DataFrame(
        class_rows
    )

    class_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_class_drift.csv"
    )

    class_df.to_csv(
        class_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"Saved:\n{feature_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"Saved:\n{stability_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"Saved:\n{class_path}"
    )

    print(
        "\n[5/5] Temporal drift summary:"
    )

    print()

    print(
        summary_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nTop-K stability:"
    )

    print()

    print(
        stability_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nClass-specific drift:"
    )

    print()

    print(
        class_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\n"
        + "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "TEMPORAL EXIFFI "
        "DRIFT ANALYSIS COMPLETE"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 70
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
=== FILE: ./scripts/evaluate_exiffi_fidelity_drift_relationship.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Tuple

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy.stats import pearsonr, spearmanr


# ============================================================
# Configuration
# ============================================================

DEFAULT_RESULTS_DIR = Path("results/tables")

FIDELITY_FILE = (
    "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_metric_sufficiency_summary.csv"
)

DRIFT_FILE = (
    "static_if_exiffi_temporal_drift_summary.csv"
)

CLASS_DRIFT_FILE = (
    "static_if_exiffi_temporal_class_drift_significance.csv"
)

DEFAULT_BOOTSTRAP = 2000
DEFAULT_RANDOM_STATE = 42

FIDELITY_METRIC = (
    "exiffi_mean_relative_score_change"
)

DRIFT_METRICS = [
    "js_divergence",
    "cosine_distance",
    "l1_distance",
    "l2_distance",
    "spearman_rank_correlation",
]


# ============================================================
# Statistical utilities
# ============================================================

def safe_spearman(
    x: np.ndarray,
    y: np.ndarray,
) -> float:

    if len(x) < 2:
        return np.nan

    if np.std(x) == 0 or np.std(y) == 0:
        return np.nan

    result = spearmanr(x, y)

    value = result.statistic

    if np.isnan(value):
        return np.nan

    return float(value)


def safe_pearson(
    x: np.ndarray,
    y: np.ndarray,
) -> float:

    if len(x) < 2:
        return np.nan

    if np.std(x) == 0 or np.std(y) == 0:
        return np.nan

    result = pearsonr(x, y)

    value = result.statistic

    if np.isnan(value):
        return np.nan

    return float(value)


# ============================================================
# Bootstrap correlation
# ============================================================

def bootstrap_correlation(
    x: np.ndarray,
    y: np.ndarray,
    method: str,
    n_bootstrap: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> Tuple[float, float, float, float]:

    observed = (
        safe_spearman(x, y)
        if method == "spearman"
        else safe_pearson(x, y)
    )

    if np.isnan(observed):
        return (
            np.nan,
            np.nan,
            np.nan,
            np.nan,
        )

    n = len(x)

    values: List[float] = []

    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):

        indices = rng.integers(
            0,
            n,
            size=n,
        )

        xb = x[indices]
        yb = y[indices]

        if method == "spearman":
            value = safe_spearman(
                xb,
                yb,
            )
        else:
            value = safe_pearson(
                xb,
                yb,
            )

        if not np.isnan(value):
            values.append(value)

    if not values:
        return (
            observed,
            np.nan,
            np.nan,
            np.nan,
        )

    values = np.asarray(values)

    lower = float(
        np.percentile(
            values,
            2.5,
        )
    )

    upper = float(
        np.percentile(
            values,
            97.5,
        )
    )

    if observed >= 0:

        p_value = (
            2.0
            * np.mean(
                values <= 0
            )
        )

    else:

        p_value = (
            2.0
            * np.mean(
                values >= 0
            )
        )

    p_value = min(
        max(float(p_value), 0.0),
        1.0,
    )

    return (
        float(observed),
        lower,
        upper,
        p_value,
    )


# ============================================================
# Loading
# ============================================================

def load_fidelity_summary(
    results_dir: Path,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    path = (
        results_dir
        / FIDELITY_FILE
    )

    if not path.exists():

        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Missing fidelity summary:\n"
            f"{path}\n\n"
            "Run E4.2-T04 first."
        )

    df = pd.read_csv(path)

    required = [
        "window",
        "top_k",
        "mean_top_k_mass",
        FIDELITY_METRIC,
    ]

    missing = [
        column
        for column in required
        if column not in df.columns
    ]

    if missing:

        raise ValueError(
            "Fidelity summary is missing "
            f"required columns: {missing}"
        )

    return df


def load_drift_summary(
    results_dir: Path,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    path = (
        results_dir
        / DRIFT_FILE
    )

    if not path.exists():

        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Missing temporal drift summary:\n"
            f"{path}\n\n"
            "Run the temporal ExIFFI drift "
            "analysis first."
        )

    df = pd.read_csv(path)

    required = [
        "comparison",
        "window_a",
        "window_b",
        *DRIFT_METRICS,
    ]

    missing = [
        column
        for column in required
        if column not in df.columns
    ]

    if missing:

        raise ValueError(
            "Temporal drift summary is missing "
            f"required columns: {missing}"
        )

    return df


# ============================================================
# Fidelity preparation
# ============================================================

def prepare_fidelity_dataframe(
    fidelity_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    result = fidelity_df[
        [
            "window",
            "top_k",
            "mean_top_k_mass",
            FIDELITY_METRIC,
        ]
    ].copy()

    result = result.rename(
        columns={
            FIDELITY_METRIC:
                "fidelity_score_change"
        }
    )

    return result


# ============================================================
# Pairwise fidelity-drift dataset
# ============================================================

def build_pairwise_dataset(
    fidelity_df: pd.DataFrame,
    drift_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    fidelity = prepare_fidelity_dataframe(
        fidelity_df
    )

    rows = []

    for _, drift_row in drift_df.iterrows():

        window_a = int(
            drift_row["window_a"]
        )

        window_b = int(
            drift_row["window_b"]
        )

        comparison = str(
            drift_row["comparison"]
        )

        candidates = fidelity[
            fidelity["window"].isin(
                [
                    window_a,
                    window_b,
                ]
            )
        ]

        if candidates.empty:
            continue

        # Prefer the fidelity measured at the
        # beginning of the temporal interval.
        start_values = candidates[
            candidates["window"]
            == window_a
        ]

        if not start_values.empty:
            selected = start_values
        else:
            selected = candidates

        for _, fidelity_row in selected.iterrows():

            row = {
                "comparison": comparison,
                "window_a": window_a,
                "window_b": window_b,
                "top_k": int(
                    fidelity_row["top_k"]
                ),
                "mean_top_k_mass": float(
                    fidelity_row[
                        "mean_top_k_mass"
                    ]
                ),
                "fidelity_score_change": float(
                    fidelity_row[
                        "fidelity_score_change"
                    ]
                ),
            }

            for metric in DRIFT_METRICS:

                row[metric] = float(
                    drift_row[metric]
                )

            rows.append(row)

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ============================================================
# Fidelity vs Top-K relationship
# ============================================================

def calculate_fidelity_k_relationship(
    fidelity_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    fidelity = prepare_fidelity_dataframe(
        fidelity_df
    )

    rows = []

    for window, group in (
        fidelity.groupby("window")
    ):

        group = (
            group
            .sort_values("top_k")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        if len(group) < 2:
            continue

        k = group[
            "top_k"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        fidelity_values = group[
            "fidelity_score_change"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        mass = group[
            "mean_top_k_mass"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        spearman_k = safe_spearman(
            k,
            fidelity_values,
        )

        spearman_mass = safe_spearman(
            mass,
            fidelity_values,
        )

        differences = np.diff(
            fidelity_values
        )

        monotonic = bool(
            np.all(
                differences >= -1e-12
            )
        )

        rows.append(
            {
                "window": int(window),
                "n_k_values": len(group),
                "fidelity_monotonic_with_k":
                    monotonic,
                "fidelity_k_spearman":
                    spearman_k,
                "fidelity_mass_spearman":
                    spearman_mass,
                "minimum_fidelity":
                    float(
                        np.min(
                            fidelity_values
                        )
                    ),
                "maximum_fidelity":
                    float(
                        np.max(
                            fidelity_values
                        )
                    ),
            }
        )

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ============================================================
# Correlation analysis
# ============================================================

def evaluate_correlations(
    pairwise_df: pd.DataFrame,
    n_bootstrap: int,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> Tuple[
    pd.DataFrame,
    pd.DataFrame,
]:

    correlation_rows = []
    bootstrap_rows = []

    if pairwise_df.empty:
        return (
            pd.DataFrame(),
            pd.DataFrame(),
        )

    # Important:
    #
    # We require at least 3 independent temporal
    # observations before calculating a correlation.
    #
    # At the current stage E4.2-T04 only provides
    # Window 1 fidelity. Therefore we should NOT
    # fabricate a correlation from repeated Top-K
    # values within the same temporal comparison.

    for top_k, group in (
        pairwise_df.groupby("top_k")
    ):

        unique_comparisons = (
            group[
                "comparison"
            ].nunique()
        )

        if unique_comparisons < 3:
            continue

        fidelity = group[
            "fidelity_score_change"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        for metric in DRIFT_METRICS:

            drift = group[
                metric
            ].to_numpy(
                dtype=float
            )

            valid = (
                np.isfinite(fidelity)
                &
                np.isfinite(drift)
            )

            fidelity_valid = (
                fidelity[valid]
            )

            drift_valid = (
                drift[valid]
            )

            if len(
                fidelity_valid
            ) < 3:

                continue

            pearson = safe_pearson(
                fidelity_valid,
                drift_valid,
            )

            spearman = safe_spearman(
                fidelity_valid,
                drift_valid,
            )

            correlation_rows.append(
                {
                    "top_k": int(top_k),
                    "drift_metric": metric,
                    "n_observations":
                        len(
                            fidelity_valid
                        ),
                    "n_temporal_comparisons":
                        unique_comparisons,
                    "pearson_correlation":
                        pearson,
                    "spearman_correlation":
                        spearman,
                }
            )

            for method in [
                "spearman",
                "pearson",
            ]:

                (
                    observed,
                    lower,
                    upper,
                    p_value,
                ) = bootstrap_correlation(
                    x=fidelity_valid,
                    y=drift_valid,
                    method=method,
                    n_bootstrap=n_bootstrap,
                    rng=rng,
                )

                bootstrap_rows.append(
                    {
                        "top_k": int(top_k),
                        "drift_metric": metric,
                        "method": method,
                        "observed_correlation":
                            observed,
                        "bootstrap_ci_lower":
                            lower,
                        "bootstrap_ci_upper":
                            upper,
                        "bootstrap_pvalue":
                            p_value,
                        "n_observations":
                            len(
                                fidelity_valid
                            ),
                        "n_temporal_comparisons":
                            unique_comparisons,
                        "n_bootstrap":
                            n_bootstrap,
                    }
                )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(
            correlation_rows
        ),
        pd.DataFrame(
            bootstrap_rows
        ),
    )


# ============================================================
# Decision
# ============================================================

def build_decision(
    pairwise_df: pd.DataFrame,
    bootstrap_df: pd.DataFrame,
    fidelity_k_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    temporal_comparisons = 0

    if not pairwise_df.empty:

        temporal_comparisons = (
            pairwise_df[
                "comparison"
            ].nunique()
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Current evidence is insufficient if fidelity exists
    # for fewer than 3 temporal comparisons.
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if temporal_comparisons < 3:

        return pd.DataFrame(
            [
                {
                    "decision":
                        "TEMPORAL_FIDELITY_EXTENSION_REQUIRED",
                    "temporal_comparisons":
                        temporal_comparisons,
                    "n_pairwise_records":
                        len(pairwise_df),
                    "significant_bootstrap_associations":
                        0,
                    "tested_bootstrap_associations":
                        0,
                    "fidelity_monotonic_with_k":
                        (
                            bool(
                                fidelity_k_df[
                                    "fidelity_monotonic_with_k"
                                ].all()
                            )
                            if not fidelity_k_df.empty
                            else False
                        ),
                    "evidence":
                        (
                            "E4.2-T04 successfully validated "
                            "the fidelity metric, but fidelity "
                            "has not yet been measured across "
                            "enough independent temporal windows "
                            "to test a fidelity-drift relationship."
                        ),
                    "recommendation":
                        (
                            "Extend E4.2-T04 to Windows 2, 3 "
                            "and 4 using the same validated "
                            "procedure before performing the "
                            "temporal fidelity-drift analysis."
                        ),
                }
            ]
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Evaluate statistical evidence.
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    significant = 0
    tested = 0

    if not bootstrap_df.empty:

        spearman_df = bootstrap_df[
            bootstrap_df["method"]
            == "spearman"
        ]

        for _, row in spearman_df.iterrows():

            tested += 1

            lower = row[
                "bootstrap_ci_lower"
            ]

            upper = row[
                "bootstrap_ci_upper"
            ]

            if (
                np.isfinite(lower)
                and np.isfinite(upper)
                and (
                    lower > 0
                    or upper < 0
                )
            ):

                significant += 1

    monotonic = False

    if not fidelity_k_df.empty:

        monotonic = bool(
            fidelity_k_df[
                "fidelity_monotonic_with_k"
            ].all()
        )

    if (
        tested > 0
        and significant > 0
        and monotonic
    ):

        decision = (
            "FIDELITY_DRIFT_RELATIONSHIP_SUPPORTED"
        )

        evidence = (
            "The validated fidelity metric shows "
            "a statistically supported association "
            "with temporal explanation drift."
        )

    else:

        decision = (
            "FIDELITY_DRIFT_RELATIONSHIP_NOT_ESTABLISHED"
        )

        evidence = (
            "The available temporal evidence does not "
            "provide sufficient statistical support for "
            "a fidelity-drift relationship."
        )

    return pd.DataFrame(
        [
            {
                "decision": decision,
                "temporal_comparisons":
                    temporal_comparisons,
                "n_pairwise_records":
                    len(pairwise_df),
                "significant_bootstrap_associations":
                    significant,
                "tested_bootstrap_associations":
                    tested,
                "fidelity_monotonic_with_k":
                    monotonic,
                "evidence": evidence,
                "recommendation":
                    (
                        "Proceed to interpretation "
                        "only if the relationship is "
                        "supported across independent "
                        "temporal comparisons."
                    ),
            }
        ]
    )


# ============================================================
# Main experiment
# ============================================================

def run_experiment(
    results_dir: Path = DEFAULT_RESULTS_DIR,
    n_bootstrap: int = DEFAULT_BOOTSTRAP,
    random_state: int = DEFAULT_RANDOM_STATE,
):

    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        "E4.2-T05 — EXIFFI FIDELITY–DRIFT RELATIONSHIP"
    )

    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        f"\nBootstrap samples: {n_bootstrap}"
    )

    print(
        f"Random state: {random_state}"
    )

    print(
        "\nPrimary fidelity metric:"
    )

    print(
        f"  {FIDELITY_METRIC}"
    )

    rng = np.random.default_rng(
        random_state
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Fidelity
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[1/7] Loading validated fidelity results..."
    )

    fidelity_df = load_fidelity_summary(
        results_dir
    )

    print(
        f"Fidelity records: "
        f"{len(fidelity_df):,}"
    )

    print(
        "Fidelity windows:",
        sorted(
            fidelity_df[
                "window"
            ].unique()
            .tolist()
        ),
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Drift
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[2/7] Loading temporal drift results..."
    )

    drift_df = load_drift_summary(
        results_dir
    )

    print(
        f"Temporal drift comparisons: "
        f"{len(drift_df):,}"
    )

    print(
        "Comparisons:",
        drift_df[
            "comparison"
        ].tolist(),
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Pairing
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[3/7] Constructing fidelity–drift dataset..."
    )

    pairwise_df = build_pairwise_dataset(
        fidelity_df=fidelity_df,
        drift_df=drift_df,
    )

    if pairwise_df.empty:

        print(
            "No valid fidelity–drift pairs found."
        )

    else:

        print(
            f"Pairwise records: "
            f"{len(pairwise_df):,}"
        )

        print(
            "Top-k values:",
            sorted(
                pairwise_df[
                    "top_k"
                ].unique()
                .tolist()
            ),
        )

        print(
            "Temporal comparisons:",
            pairwise_df[
                "comparison"
            ].nunique(),
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Correlations
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[4/7] Evaluating fidelity–drift associations..."
    )

    (
        correlation_df,
        bootstrap_df,
    ) = evaluate_correlations(
        pairwise_df=pairwise_df,
        n_bootstrap=n_bootstrap,
        rng=rng,
    )

    if correlation_df.empty:

        print(
            "  Correlations not estimable."
        )

        print(
            "  Reason: fewer than 3 independent "
            "temporal comparisons have fidelity data."
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Fidelity behaviour
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[5/7] Evaluating fidelity behaviour..."
    )

    fidelity_k_df = (
        calculate_fidelity_k_relationship(
            fidelity_df
        )
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Decision
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[6/7] Assessing temporal evidence..."
    )

    decision_df = build_decision(
        pairwise_df=pairwise_df,
        bootstrap_df=bootstrap_df,
        fidelity_k_df=fidelity_k_df,
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Save
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[7/7] Saving fidelity–drift results..."
    )

    results_dir.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    pairwise_path = (
        results_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_drift_relationship.csv"
    )

    correlation_path = (
        results_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_drift_correlation.csv"
    )

    bootstrap_path = (
        results_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_drift_bootstrap.csv"
    )

    fidelity_k_path = (
        results_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_k_relationship.csv"
    )

    decision_path = (
        results_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_drift_decision.csv"
    )

    pairwise_df.to_csv(
        pairwise_path,
        index=False,
    )

    correlation_df.to_csv(
        correlation_path,
        index=False,
    )

    bootstrap_df.to_csv(
        bootstrap_path,
        index=False,
    )

    fidelity_k_df.to_csv(
        fidelity_k_path,
        index=False,
    )

    decision_df.to_csv(
        decision_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        "\nResults saved:"
    )

    print(
        f"  Fidelity–drift relationship:\n"
        f"    {pairwise_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Correlations:\n"
        f"    {correlation_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Bootstrap:\n"
        f"    {bootstrap_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Fidelity–K relationship:\n"
        f"    {fidelity_k_path}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Decision:\n"
        f"    {decision_path}"
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Summary
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n"
        + "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "E4.2-T05 — FIDELITY–DRIFT SUMMARY"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "\nCorrelation analysis:"
    )

    if correlation_df.empty:

        print(
            "  Not estimable with the current "
            "temporal fidelity coverage."
        )

    else:

        print(
            correlation_df.to_string(
                index=False
            )
        )

    print(
        "\nFidelity behaviour:"
    )

    if fidelity_k_df.empty:

        print(
            "  Not available."
        )

    else:

        print(
            fidelity_k_df.to_string(
                index=False
            )
        )

    print(
        "\nDecision:"
    )

    print(
        decision_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\n"
        + "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "E4.2-T05 COMPLETE"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 70
    )

    return (
        pairwise_df,
        correlation_df,
        bootstrap_df,
        fidelity_k_df,
        decision_df,
    )


# ============================================================
# CLI
# ============================================================

def main():

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Evaluate the relationship between "
            "ExIFFI explanation fidelity and "
            "temporal explanation drift."
        )
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--results-dir",
        default=str(
            DEFAULT_RESULTS_DIR
        ),
        help=(
            "Directory containing generated "
            "experimental results."
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--bootstrap",
        type=int,
        default=DEFAULT_BOOTSTRAP,
        help=(
            "Number of bootstrap samples."
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--random-state",
        type=int,
        default=DEFAULT_RANDOM_STATE,
        help=(
            "Random seed."
        ),
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    run_experiment(
        results_dir=Path(
            args.results_dir
        ),
        n_bootstrap=args.bootstrap,
        random_state=args.random_state,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy.stats import pearsonr, spearmanr

# Configuration
# ============================================================

DEFAULT_FIDELITY_FILE = (
    DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR
    / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_fidelity_trajectory.csv"
)

DEFAULT_CLASS_FIDELITY_FILE = (
    DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR
    / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_fidelity_class_trajectory.csv"
)

DEFAULT_DETECTION_FILE = (
    DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR
    / "static_if_temporal_model_performance.csv"

DEFAULT_ALPHA = 0.05

WINDOWS = [1, 2, 3, 4]

    {

        "f1": [
            0.269652,
            0.238556,
            0.219187,
            0.203904,
        ],
        "pr_auc": [
            0.214765,
            0.213656,

# ============================================================
# Utility
# ============================================================


def safe_relative_change(
    current: float,
    baseline: float,
) -> float:

    if abs(baseline) < 1e-12:
        return np.nan

    return (
        current - baseline
    ) / abs(baseline)


def safe_percentage_change(
    current: float,
    baseline: float,
) -> float:

    value = safe_relative_change(
        current,
        baseline,
    )

    if np.isnan(value):
        return np.nan

    return value * 100.0


def minmax_normalize(
    values: pd.Series,
    higher_is_worse: bool,
) -> pd.Series:

    values = values.astype(float)

    minimum = values.min()
    maximum = values.max()

    if np.isclose(
        minimum,
        maximum,
    ):
        result = pd.Series(
            np.zeros(
                len(values)
            ),
            index=values.index,
        )
    else:
        result = (
            values - minimum
        ) / (
            maximum - minimum
        )

    if higher_is_worse:
        return result

    return 1.0 - result


# ============================================================
# Load fidelity trajectory
# ============================================================


def load_fidelity_trajectory(
    path: Path,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    if not path.exists():

        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "\n"
            "Temporal fidelity trajectory "
            "was not found:\n"
            f"{path}\n\n"
            "Run the temporal fidelity "
            "evaluation first."
        )

    df = pd.read_csv(path)

    required = {
        "window",
        "top_k",
        "mean_relative_score_change",
        "window_to_window_change",
        "change_from_window_1",
    }

    missing = (
        required
        - set(df.columns)
    )

    if missing:

        raise ValueError(
            "Temporal fidelity trajectory "
            "is missing required columns: "
            f"{sorted(missing)}"
        )

    df["window"] = (
        df["window"]
        .astype(int)
    )

    df["top_k"] = (
        df["top_k"]
        .astype(int)
    )

    return (
        df
        .sort_values(
            [
                "top_k",
                "window",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# Load class-specific fidelity
# ============================================================


def load_class_fidelity(
    path: Path,
) -> pd.DataFrame | None:

    if not path.exists():
        return None

    df = pd.read_csv(path)

    required = {
        "window",
        "isFraud",
        "class",
        "top_k",
        "mean_relative_score_change",
    }

    missing = (
        required
        - set(df.columns)
    )

    if missing:

        raise ValueError(
            "Class fidelity trajectory "
            "is missing required columns: "
            f"{sorted(missing)}"
        )

    df["window"] = (
        df["window"]
        .astype(int)
    )

    df["top_k"] = (
        df["top_k"]
        .astype(int)
    )

    df["isFraud"] = (
        df["isFraud"]
        .astype(int)
    )

    return (
        df
        .sort_values(
            [
                "class",
                "top_k",
                "window",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# Detection metrics
# ============================================================


def load_detection_results(
    path: Path | None,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    if path is None:

        print(
            "\nNo detection trajectory CSV "
            "supplied."
        )

        print(
            "Using the documented baseline "
            "detection trajectory."
        )

        return (
            DEFAULT_DETECTION_RESULTS
            .copy()
        )

    if not path.exists():

        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "Detection results file not found:\n"
            f"{path}"
        )

    df = pd.read_csv(path)

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Flexible column recognition.
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    aliases = {
        "window": [
            "window",
            "Window",
        ],
        "f1": [
            "f1",
            "F1",
            "f1_score",
            "F1_score",
        ],
        "pr_auc": [
            "pr_auc",
            "PR_AUC",
            "pr-auc",
            "PR-AUC",
            "average_precision",
        ],
        "fraud_rate": [
            "fraud_rate",
            "Fraud rate",
            "fraudRate",
        ],
    }

    rename_map = {}

    for canonical, candidates in aliases.items():

        for candidate in candidates:

            if candidate in df.columns:

                rename_map[
                    candidate
                ] = canonical

                break

    df = df.rename(
        columns=rename_map
    )

    required = {
        "window",
        "f1",
        "pr_auc",
    }

    missing = (
        required
        - set(df.columns)
    )

    if missing:

        raise ValueError(
            "Detection results are missing "
            "required columns: "
            f"{sorted(missing)}"
        )

    df["window"] = (
        df["window"]
        .astype(int)
    )

    return (
        df
        .sort_values("window")
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# Detection trajectory analysis
# ============================================================


def calculate_detection_trajectory(
    detection_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    df = (
        detection_df
        .copy()
        .sort_values("window")
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

    baseline_f1 = float(
        df.iloc[0]["f1"]
    )

    baseline_pr_auc = float(
        df.iloc[0]["pr_auc"]
    )

    previous_f1 = None
    previous_pr_auc = None

    rows = []

    for _, row in df.iterrows():

        window = int(
            row["window"]
        )

        f1 = float(
            row["f1"]
        )

        pr_auc = float(
            row["pr_auc"]
        )

        if previous_f1 is None:

            f1_window_change = 0.0
            pr_auc_window_change = 0.0

        else:

            f1_window_change = (
                f1
                - previous_f1
            )

            pr_auc_window_change = (
                pr_auc
                - previous_pr_auc
            )

        rows.append(
            {
                "window": window,

                "f1": f1,

                "pr_auc": pr_auc,

                "f1_change_from_window_1": (
                    f1
                    - baseline_f1
                ),

                "pr_auc_change_from_window_1": (
                    pr_auc
                    - baseline_pr_auc
                ),

                "f1_relative_change_pct": (
                    safe_percentage_change(
                        f1,
                        baseline_f1,
                    )
                ),

                "pr_auc_relative_change_pct": (
                    safe_percentage_change(
                        pr_auc,
                        baseline_pr_auc,
                    )
                ),

                "f1_window_to_window_change": (
                    f1_window_change
                ),

                "pr_auc_window_to_window_change": (
                    pr_auc_window_change
                ),

                "f1_relative_degradation_pct": (
                    -safe_percentage_change(
                        f1,
                        baseline_f1,
                    )
                ),

                "pr_auc_relative_degradation_pct": (
                    -safe_percentage_change(
                        pr_auc,
                        baseline_pr_auc,
                    )
                ),
            }
        )

        previous_f1 = f1
        previous_pr_auc = pr_auc

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ============================================================
# Fidelity trajectory analysis
# ============================================================


def calculate_fidelity_trajectory(
    fidelity_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    rows = []

    for top_k, group in fidelity_df.groupby(
        "top_k"
    ):

        group = (
            group
            .sort_values("window")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        baseline = float(
            group.iloc[0][
                "mean_relative_score_change"
            ]
        )

        previous = None

        for _, row in group.iterrows():

            window = int(
                row["window"]
            )

            fidelity = float(
                row[
                    "mean_relative_score_change"
                ]
            )

            if previous is None:

                window_change = 0.0

            else:

                window_change = (
                    fidelity
                    - previous
                )

            rows.append(
                {
                    "window": window,

                    "top_k": int(
                        top_k
                    ),

                    "fidelity": fidelity,

                    "fidelity_change_from_window_1": (
                        fidelity
                        - baseline
                    ),

                    "fidelity_relative_change_pct": (
                        safe_percentage_change(
                            fidelity,
                            baseline,
                        )
                    ),

                    "fidelity_window_to_window_change": (
                        window_change
                    ),
                }
            )

            previous = fidelity

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(rows)
        .sort_values(
            [
                "top_k",
                "window",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# Merge detection + fidelity
# ============================================================


def build_integrated_trajectory(
    fidelity_trajectory: pd.DataFrame,
    detection_trajectory: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    rows = []

    for top_k, fidelity_group in (
        fidelity_trajectory.groupby(
            "top_k"
        )
    ):

        merged = fidelity_group.merge(
            detection_trajectory,
            on="window",
            how="left",
            validate="one_to_one",
        )

        merged[
            "top_k"
        ] = int(top_k)

        rows.append(
            merged
        )

    if not rows:

        return pd.DataFrame()

    return (
        pd.concat(
            rows,
            ignore_index=True,
        )
        .sort_values(
            [
                "top_k",
                "window",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# Dissociation metrics
# ============================================================


def calculate_dissociation(
    integrated_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    rows = []

    for top_k, group in integrated_df.groupby(
        "top_k"
    ):

        group = (
            group
            .sort_values("window")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        for _, row in group.iterrows():

            # Detection degradation is positive
            # when F1/PR-AUC decrease.
            f1_degradation = float(
                row[
                    "f1_relative_degradation_pct"
                ]
            )

            pr_auc_degradation = float(
                row[
                    "pr_auc_relative_degradation_pct"
                ]
            )

            # Fidelity loss is represented as
            # negative fidelity change from
            # Window 1.
            fidelity_change = float(
                row[
                    "fidelity_relative_change_pct"
                ]
            )

            fidelity_degradation = (
                -fidelity_change
            )

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # Dissociation:
            #
            # positive:
            # detection deteriorates more than fidelity
            #
            # negative:
            # fidelity deteriorates more than detection
            # ------------------------------------------------

            dissociation_f1 = (
                fidelity_degradation
                - f1_degradation
            )

            dissociation_pr_auc = (
                fidelity_degradation
                - pr_auc_degradation
            )

            rows.append(
                {
                    "window": int(
                        row["window"]
                    ),

                    "top_k": int(
                        top_k
                    ),

                    "fidelity_degradation_pct": (
                        fidelity_degradation
                    ),

                    "f1_degradation_pct": (
                        f1_degradation
                    ),

                    "pr_auc_degradation_pct": (
                        pr_auc_degradation
                    ),

                    "fidelity_minus_f1_degradation": (
                        dissociation_f1
                    ),

                    "fidelity_minus_pr_auc_degradation": (
                        dissociation_pr_auc
                    ),

                    "absolute_f1_fidelity_dissociation": (
                        abs(
                            dissociation_f1
                        )
                    ),

                    "absolute_pr_auc_fidelity_dissociation": (
                        abs(
                            dissociation_pr_auc
                        )
                    ),
                }
            )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(rows)
        .sort_values(
            [
                "top_k",
                "window",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# Fidelity Drift Index
# ============================================================


def calculate_fidelity_drift_index(
    fidelity_trajectory: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    rows = []

    for top_k, group in fidelity_trajectory.groupby(
        "top_k"
    ):

        group = (
            group
            .sort_values("window")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        baseline = float(
            group.iloc[0][
                "fidelity"
            ]
        )

        for _, row in group.iterrows():

            current = float(
                row["fidelity"]
            )

            # ------------------------------------------------
            # The fidelity measure is score retention.
            #
            # Lower retention means greater
            # explanation-fidelity degradation.
            # ------------------------------------------------

            if abs(baseline) < 1e-12:

                relative_degradation = np.nan

            else:

                relative_degradation = (
                    (
                        baseline
                        - current
                    )
                    / abs(baseline)
                )

            rows.append(
                {
                    "window": int(
                        row["window"]
                    ),

                    "top_k": int(
                        top_k
                    ),

                    "fidelity_drift_index": (
                        relative_degradation
                    ),

                    "fidelity_drift_index_pct": (
                        relative_degradation
                        * 100.0
                        if not np.isnan(
                            relative_degradation
                        )
                        else np.nan
                    ),
                }
            )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(rows)
        .sort_values(
            [
                "top_k",
                "window",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# Temporal slopes
# ============================================================


def calculate_temporal_slopes(
    integrated_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    rows = []

    for top_k, group in integrated_df.groupby(
        "top_k"
    ):

        group = (
            group
            .sort_values("window")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        x = group[
            "window"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        y_fidelity = group[
            "fidelity"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        y_f1 = group[
            "f1"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        y_pr_auc = group[
            "pr_auc"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        fidelity_slope = np.polyfit(
            x,
            y_fidelity,
            1,
        )[0]

        f1_slope = np.polyfit(
            x,
            y_f1,
            1,
        )[0]

        pr_auc_slope = np.polyfit(
            x,
            y_pr_auc,
            1,
        )[0]

        rows.append(
            {
                "top_k": int(
                    top_k
                ),

                "fidelity_temporal_slope": (
                    fidelity_slope
                ),

                "f1_temporal_slope": (
                    f1_slope
                ),

                "pr_auc_temporal_slope": (
                    pr_auc_slope
                ),
            }
        )

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ============================================================
# Correlation analysis
# ============================================================


def calculate_correlations(
    integrated_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    rows = []

    for top_k, group in integrated_df.groupby(
        "top_k"
    ):

        group = (
            group
            .sort_values("window")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        fidelity = group[
            "fidelity"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        f1 = group[
            "f1"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        pr_auc = group[
            "pr_auc"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # With only four temporal windows, correlation
        # coefficients are descriptive rather than
        # definitive inferential evidence.
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        if len(fidelity) >= 3:

            pearson_f1, pearson_f1_p = (
                pearsonr(
                    fidelity,
                    f1,
                )
            )

            spearman_f1, spearman_f1_p = (
                spearmanr(
                    fidelity,
                    f1,
                )
            )

            pearson_pr, pearson_pr_p = (
                pearsonr(
                    fidelity,
                    pr_auc,
                )
            )

            spearman_pr, spearman_pr_p = (
                spearmanr(
                    fidelity,
                    pr_auc,
                )
            )

        else:

            pearson_f1 = np.nan
            pearson_f1_p = np.nan
            spearman_f1 = np.nan
            spearman_f1_p = np.nan

            pearson_pr = np.nan
            pearson_pr_p = np.nan
            spearman_pr = np.nan
            spearman_pr_p = np.nan

        rows.append(
            {
                "top_k": int(
                    top_k
                ),

                "pearson_fidelity_f1": (
                    pearson_f1
                ),

                "pearson_fidelity_f1_pvalue": (
                    pearson_f1_p
                ),

                "spearman_fidelity_f1": (
                    spearman_f1
                ),

                "spearman_fidelity_f1_pvalue": (
                    spearman_f1_p
                ),

                "pearson_fidelity_pr_auc": (
                    pearson_pr
                ),

                "pearson_fidelity_pr_auc_pvalue": (
                    pearson_pr_p
                ),

                "spearman_fidelity_pr_auc": (
                    spearman_pr
                ),

                "spearman_fidelity_pr_auc_pvalue": (
                    spearman_pr_p
                ),
            }
        )

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ============================================================
# Summary
# ============================================================


def build_summary(
    integrated_df: pd.DataFrame,
    drift_index_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    rows = []

    for top_k, group in integrated_df.groupby(
        "top_k"
    ):

        group = (
            group
            .sort_values("window")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        drift_group = (
            drift_index_df[
                drift_index_df[
                    "top_k"
                ]
                == top_k
            ]
            .sort_values("window")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        final = group.iloc[-1]
        final_drift = drift_group.iloc[-1]

        rows.append(
            {
                "top_k": int(
