                    top_k
                ),

                "window_1_fidelity": (
                    group.iloc[0][
                        "fidelity"
                    ]
                ),

                "window_4_fidelity": (
                    final[
                        "fidelity"
                    ]
                ),

                "absolute_fidelity_change": (
                    final[
                        "fidelity"
                    ]
                    - group.iloc[0][
                        "fidelity"
                    ]
                ),

                "relative_fidelity_change_pct": (
                    final[
                        "fidelity_relative_change_pct"
                    ]
                ),

                "fidelity_drift_index_pct": (
                    final_drift[
                        "fidelity_drift_index_pct"
                    ]
                ),

                "window_1_f1": (
                    group.iloc[0][
                        "f1"
                    ]
                ),

                "window_4_f1": (
                    final[
                        "f1"
                    ]
                ),

                "f1_relative_degradation_pct": (
                    final[
                        "f1_relative_degradation_pct"
                    ]
                ),

                "window_1_pr_auc": (
                    group.iloc[0][
                        "pr_auc"
                    ]
                ),

                "window_4_pr_auc": (
                    final[
                        "pr_auc"
                    ]
                ),

                "pr_auc_relative_degradation_pct": (
                    final[
                        "pr_auc_relative_degradation_pct"
                    ]
                ),
            }
        )

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ============================================================
# Research interpretation
# ============================================================


def generate_research_interpretation(
    summary_df: pd.DataFrame,
    correlation_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    rows = []

    for _, row in summary_df.iterrows():

        top_k = int(
            row["top_k"]
        )

        fidelity_change = float(
            row[
                "relative_fidelity_change_pct"
            ]
        )

        f1_degradation = float(
            row[
                "f1_relative_degradation_pct"
            ]
        )

        pr_auc_degradation = float(
            row[
                "pr_auc_relative_degradation_pct"
            ]
        )

        corr = correlation_df[
            correlation_df[
                "top_k"
            ]
            == top_k
        ]

        if len(corr) == 1:

            corr = corr.iloc[0]

            spearman_f1 = float(
                corr[
                    "spearman_fidelity_f1"
                ]
            )

            spearman_pr_auc = float(
                corr[
                    "spearman_fidelity_pr_auc"
                ]
            )

        else:

            spearman_f1 = np.nan
            spearman_pr_auc = np.nan

        if fidelity_change < 0:

            fidelity_direction = (
                "fidelity decreased"
            )

        elif fidelity_change > 0:

            fidelity_direction = (
                "fidelity increased"
            )

        else:

            fidelity_direction = (
                "fidelity remained unchanged"
            )

        if (
            f1_degradation > 0
            and pr_auc_degradation > 0
        ):

            detection_direction = (
                "both F1 and PR-AUC degraded"
            )

        elif (
            f1_degradation > 0
            or pr_auc_degradation > 0
        ):

            detection_direction = (
                "detection performance partially degraded"
            )

        else:

            detection_direction = (
                "detection performance did not degrade"
            )

        rows.append(
            {
                "top_k": top_k,

                "window_1_to_window_4": (
                    f"{fidelity_direction}; "
                    f"{detection_direction}"
                ),

                "fidelity_change_pct": (
                    fidelity_change
                ),

                "f1_degradation_pct": (
                    f1_degradation
                ),

                "pr_auc_degradation_pct": (
                    pr_auc_degradation
                ),

                "spearman_fidelity_f1": (
                    spearman_f1
                ),

                "spearman_fidelity_pr_auc": (
                    spearman_pr_auc
                ),

                "interpretation": (
                    "Descriptive temporal evidence only. "
                    "With four windows, this result should "
                    "not be treated as definitive inferential "
                    "evidence. It is used to identify the "
                    "temporal relationship that must be "
                    "tested in the robustness/statistical "
                    "experiments."
                ),
            }
        )

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ============================================================
# Research manifest
# ============================================================


def build_result_manifest(
    output_dir: Path,
    generated_files: List[Path],
) -> pd.DataFrame:

    rows = []

    for path in generated_files:

        rows.append(
            {
                "artifact": path.name,

                "path": str(
                    path
                ),

                "category": (
                    "E5.3 temporal "
                    "fidelity-vs-detection"
                ),

                "status": "generated",

                "purpose": (
                    "Research evidence artifact "
                    "for temporal explanation-fidelity "
                    "analysis."
                ),
            }
        )

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


# ============================================================
# Main experiment
# ============================================================


def run_experiment(
    fidelity_file: Path = DEFAULT_FIDELITY_FILE,
    detection_file: Path | None = None,
    class_fidelity_file: Path = DEFAULT_CLASS_FIDELITY_FILE,
    output_dir: Path = DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR,
):

    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        "BASELINE 1 — TEMPORAL "
        "FIDELITY VS DETECTION ANALYSIS"
    )

    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        "\nThis analysis compares:"
    )

    print(
        "  • ExIFFI decision-level fidelity"
    )

    print(
        "  • F1 trajectory"
    )

    print(
        "  • PR-AUC trajectory"
    )

    print(
        "  • quantitative fidelity drift"
    )

    print(
        "  • detection/fidelity dissociation"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Load fidelity
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[1/8] Loading fidelity trajectory..."
    )

    fidelity_df = load_fidelity_trajectory(
        fidelity_file
    )

    print(
        f"Loaded {len(fidelity_df):,} "
        "fidelity trajectory rows."
    )

    print(
        "Top-k values:",
        sorted(
            fidelity_df[
                "top_k"
            ]
            .unique()
            .tolist()
        ),
    )

    print(
        "Windows:",
        sorted(
            fidelity_df[
                "window"
            ]
            .unique()
            .tolist()
        ),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Load detection
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[2/8] Loading detection trajectory..."
    )

    detection_df = (
        load_detection_results(
            detection_file
        )
    )

    print(
        detection_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Calculate trajectories
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[3/8] Calculating temporal trajectories..."
    )

    fidelity_trajectory = (
        calculate_fidelity_trajectory(
            fidelity_df
        )
    )

    detection_trajectory = (
        calculate_detection_trajectory(
            detection_df
        )
    )

    integrated_df = (
        build_integrated_trajectory(
            fidelity_trajectory,
            detection_trajectory,
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Dissociation
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[4/8] Calculating "
        "detection/fidelity dissociation..."
    )

    dissociation_df = (
        calculate_dissociation(
            integrated_df
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Fidelity drift index
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[5/8] Calculating "
        "fidelity-drift indicators..."
    )

    drift_index_df = (
        calculate_fidelity_drift_index(
            fidelity_trajectory
        )
    )

    slope_df = (
        calculate_temporal_slopes(
            integrated_df
        )
    )

    correlation_df = (
        calculate_correlations(
            integrated_df
        )
    )

    summary_df = (
        build_summary(
            integrated_df,
            drift_index_df,
        )
    )

    interpretation_df = (
        generate_research_interpretation(
            summary_df,
            correlation_df,
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Class-specific analysis
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[6/8] Loading class-specific fidelity..."
    )

    class_fidelity_df = (
        load_class_fidelity(
            class_fidelity_file
        )
    )

    class_dissociation_df = (
        pd.DataFrame()
    )

    if class_fidelity_df is not None:

        class_fidelity_trajectory = (
            calculate_fidelity_trajectory(
                class_fidelity_df
                .rename(
                    columns={
                        "mean_relative_score_change":
                            "mean_relative_score_change"
                    }
                )
            )
        )

        # Keep class-specific information
        # directly in a dedicated table rather
        # than forcing it into the overall
        # trajectory.

        class_rows = []

        for (
            is_fraud,
            class_name,
            top_k,
        ), group in class_fidelity_df.groupby(
            [
                "isFraud",
                "class",
                "top_k",
            ]
        ):

            group = (
                group
                .sort_values("window")
                .reset_index(drop=True)
            )

            baseline = float(
                group.iloc[0][
                    "mean_relative_score_change"
                ]
            )

            for _, row in group.iterrows():

                current = float(
                    row[
                        "mean_relative_score_change"
                    ]
                )

                class_rows.append(
                    {
                        "window": int(
                            row["window"]
                        ),

                        "isFraud": int(
                            is_fraud
                        ),

                        "class": class_name,

                        "top_k": int(
                            top_k
                        ),

                        "fidelity": current,

                        "fidelity_change_from_window_1": (
                            current
                            - baseline
                        ),

                        "fidelity_relative_change_pct": (
                            safe_percentage_change(
                                current,
                                baseline,
                            )
                        ),
                    }
                )

        class_dissociation_df = (
            pd.DataFrame(
                class_rows
            )
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Save
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[7/8] Saving research artifacts..."
    )

    output_dir.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    integrated_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_fidelity_detection_trajectory.csv"
    )

    detection_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_temporal_detection_trajectory.csv"
    )

    dissociation_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_detection_dissociation.csv"
    )

    drift_index_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_drift_index.csv"
    )

    slopes_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_detection_slopes.csv"
    )

    correlations_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_detection_correlations.csv"
    )

    summary_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_detection_summary.csv"
    )

    interpretation_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_detection_interpretation.csv"
    )

    integrated_df.to_csv(
        integrated_path,
        index=False,
    )

    detection_trajectory.to_csv(
        detection_path,
        index=False,
    )

    dissociation_df.to_csv(
        dissociation_path,
        index=False,
    )

    drift_index_df.to_csv(
        drift_index_path,
        index=False,
    )

    slope_df.to_csv(
        slopes_path,
        index=False,
    )

    correlation_df.to_csv(
        correlations_path,
        index=False,
    )

    summary_df.to_csv(
        summary_path,
        index=False,
    )

    interpretation_df.to_csv(
        interpretation_path,
        index=False,
    )

    generated_files = [
        integrated_path,
        detection_path,
        dissociation_path,
        drift_index_path,
        slopes_path,
        correlations_path,
        summary_path,
        interpretation_path,
    ]

    if (
        not class_dissociation_df.empty
    ):

        class_path = (
            output_dir
            / "static_if_exiffi_class_fidelity_temporal_analysis.csv"
        )

        class_dissociation_df.to_csv(
            class_path,
            index=False,
        )

        generated_files.append(
            class_path
        )

    manifest_df = (
        build_result_manifest(
            output_dir,
            generated_files,
        )
    )

    manifest_path = (
        output_dir
        / "static_if_exiffi_fidelity_detection_result_manifest.csv"
    )

    manifest_df.to_csv(
        manifest_path,
        index=False,
    )

    generated_files.append(
        manifest_path
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Console summary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[8/8] Analysis summary"
    )

    print(
        "\nIntegrated trajectory:"
    )

    print(
        integrated_df[
            [
                "window",
                "top_k",
                "fidelity",
                "f1",
                "pr_auc",
                "fidelity_relative_change_pct",
                "f1_relative_degradation_pct",
                "pr_auc_relative_degradation_pct",
            ]
        ].to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nFidelity-drift indicators:"
    )

    print(
        drift_index_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nDetection/fidelity dissociation:"
    )

    print(
        dissociation_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nTemporal slopes:"
    )

    print(
        slope_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nCorrelations:"
    )

    print(
        correlation_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nResearch summary:"
    )

    print(
        summary_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nArtifacts saved:"
    )

    for path in generated_files:

        print(
            f"  {path}"
        )

    print(
        "\n"
        + "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "TEMPORAL FIDELITY VS DETECTION "
        "ANALYSIS COMPLETE"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 70
    )

    return (
        integrated_df,
        dissociation_df,
        drift_index_df,
        slope_df,
        correlation_df,
        summary_df,
        interpretation_df,
    )


# ============================================================
# CLI
# ============================================================


def main():

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Compare temporal ExIFFI "
            "explanation fidelity against "
            "Isolation Forest detection "
            "performance."
        )
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--fidelity-file",
        default=str(
            DEFAULT_FIDELITY_FILE
        ),
        help=(
            "Temporal ExIFFI fidelity "
            "trajectory CSV."
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--detection-file",
        default=None,
        help=(
            "Optional detection trajectory "
            "CSV containing window, F1 and "
            "PR-AUC. If omitted, the documented "
            "baseline trajectory is used."
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--class-fidelity-file",
        default=str(
            DEFAULT_CLASS_FIDELITY_FILE
        ),
        help=(
            "Class-specific temporal fidelity "
            "trajectory CSV."
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir",
        default=str(
            DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR
        ),
        help=(
            "Directory for research outputs."
        ),
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    run_experiment(
        fidelity_file=Path(
            args.fidelity_file
        ),
        detection_file=(
            Path(
                args.detection_file
            )
            if args.detection_file
            else None
        ),
        class_fidelity_file=Path(
            args.class_fidelity_file
        ),
        output_dir=Path(
            args.output_dir
        ),
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
=== FILE: ./scripts/evaluate_exiffi_random_significance.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy.stats import wilcoxon


INPUT = Path(
    "results/tables/"
    "static_if_exiffi_vs_random_fidelity.csv"
)

OUTPUT = Path(
    "results/tables/"
    "static_if_exiffi_vs_random_significance.csv"
)

DETAIL_OUTPUT = Path(
    "results/tables/"
    "static_if_exiffi_vs_random_paired_differences.csv"
)


def cliffs_delta(x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """
    Compute paired rank-based effect size.

    For paired observations, this is calculated from
    the signed differences.

    Interpretation:

        > 0  ExIFFI tends to produce larger fidelity
        < 0  Random tends to produce larger fidelity
         0  no systematic difference
    """

    differences = x - y

    differences = differences[
        differences != 0
    ]

    if len(differences) == 0:
        return 0.0

    n_positive = np.sum(
        differences > 0
    )

    n_negative = np.sum(
        differences < 0
    )

    return float(
        (n_positive - n_negative)
        / len(differences)
    )


def bootstrap_mean_difference(
    differences: np.ndarray,
    n_bootstrap: int = 10_000,
    random_state: int = 42,
) -> tuple[float, float]:

    rng = np.random.default_rng(
        random_state
    )

    n = len(differences)

    bootstrap_means = np.empty(
        n_bootstrap,
        dtype=float,
    )

    for i in range(n_bootstrap):

        sample = rng.choice(
            differences,
            size=n,
            replace=True,
        )

        bootstrap_means[i] = (
            np.mean(sample)
        )

    lower = float(
        np.percentile(
            bootstrap_means,
            2.5,
        )
    )

    upper = float(
        np.percentile(
            bootstrap_means,
            97.5,
        )
    )

    return lower, upper


def main():

    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "EXIFFI VS RANDOM — "
        "PAIRED STATISTICAL SIGNIFICANCE"
    )
    print("=" * 70)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Load results
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[1/5] Loading fidelity comparison..."
    )

    df = pd.read_csv(
        INPUT
    )

    required_columns = {
        "condition",
        "sample_index",
        "top_k",
        "mean_score_change",
    }

    # The actual input is observation-level, so
    # verify the required fields available in it.
    required_observation_columns = {
        "condition",
        "sample_index",
        "top_k",
        "score_change",
        "relative_score_change",
    }

    missing = (
        required_observation_columns
        - set(df.columns)
    )

    if missing:
        raise ValueError(
            "Input file is missing required "
            f"columns: {sorted(missing)}"
        )

    print(
        f"Rows: {len(df):,}"
    )

    print(
        "Conditions:",
        sorted(df["condition"].unique()),
    )

    print(
        "Top-k values:",
        sorted(df["top_k"].unique()),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Construct paired observations
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[2/5] Constructing paired comparisons..."
    )

    results = []
    detail_rows = []

    for top_k in sorted(
        df["top_k"].unique()
    ):

        subset = df[
            df["top_k"] == top_k
        ].copy()

        exiffi = subset[
            subset["condition"] == "exiffi"
        ][
            [
                "sample_index",
                "score_change",
                "relative_score_change",
            ]
        ].rename(
            columns={
                "score_change":
                    "exiffi_score_change",
                "relative_score_change":
                    "exiffi_relative_score_change",
            }
        )

        random = subset[
            subset["condition"] == "random"
        ][
            [
                "sample_index",
                "score_change",
                "relative_score_change",
            ]
        ].rename(
            columns={
                "score_change":
                    "random_score_change",
                "relative_score_change":
                    "random_relative_score_change",
            }
        )

        paired = exiffi.merge(
            random,
            on="sample_index",
            how="inner",
        )

        if paired.empty:
            raise ValueError(
                f"No paired observations for "
                f"top_k={top_k}"
            )

        paired[
            "score_change_difference"
        ] = (
            paired["exiffi_score_change"]
            - paired["random_score_change"]
        )

        paired[
            "relative_score_change_difference"
        ] = (
            paired[
                "exiffi_relative_score_change"
            ]
            - paired[
                "random_relative_score_change"
            ]
        )

        paired["top_k"] = top_k

        detail_rows.append(
            paired
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Statistical test: raw score change
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        exiffi_score = (
            paired[
                "exiffi_score_change"
            ].to_numpy()
        )

        random_score = (
            paired[
                "random_score_change"
            ].to_numpy()
        )

        score_difference = (
            exiffi_score
            - random_score
        )

        # Wilcoxon signed-rank test.
        #
        # Alternative hypothesis:
        #
        # ExIFFI > Random
        score_test = wilcoxon(
            exiffi_score,
            random_score,
            alternative="greater",
            zero_method="wilcox",
        )

        score_ci_lower, score_ci_upper = (
            bootstrap_mean_difference(
                score_difference
            )
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Statistical test: relative score change
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        exiffi_relative = (
            paired[
                "exiffi_relative_score_change"
            ].to_numpy()
        )

        random_relative = (
            paired[
                "random_relative_score_change"
            ].to_numpy()
        )

        relative_difference = (
            exiffi_relative
            - random_relative
        )

        relative_test = wilcoxon(
            exiffi_relative,
            random_relative,
            alternative="greater",
            zero_method="wilcox",
        )

        relative_ci_lower, relative_ci_upper = (
            bootstrap_mean_difference(
                relative_difference
            )
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Effect sizes
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        score_effect = cliffs_delta(
            exiffi_score,
            random_score,
        )

        relative_effect = cliffs_delta(
            exiffi_relative,
            random_relative,
        )

        results.append(
            {
                "top_k": top_k,
                "n_observations": len(paired),

                # Raw score change
                "mean_exiffi_score_change":
                    np.mean(exiffi_score),

                "mean_random_score_change":
                    np.mean(random_score),

                "mean_score_difference":
                    np.mean(score_difference),

                "median_score_difference":
                    np.median(score_difference),

                "score_difference_ci_lower":
                    score_ci_lower,

                "score_difference_ci_upper":
                    score_ci_upper,

                "wilcoxon_score_statistic":
                    float(score_test.statistic),

                "wilcoxon_score_pvalue":
                    float(score_test.pvalue),

                "score_effect_size":
                    score_effect,

                # Relative score change
                "mean_exiffi_relative_change":
                    np.mean(exiffi_relative),

                "mean_random_relative_change":
                    np.mean(random_relative),

                "mean_relative_difference":
                    np.mean(relative_difference),

                "median_relative_difference":
                    np.median(relative_difference),

                "relative_difference_ci_lower":
                    relative_ci_lower,

                "relative_difference_ci_upper":
                    relative_ci_upper,

                "wilcoxon_relative_statistic":
                    float(
                        relative_test.statistic
                    ),

                "wilcoxon_relative_pvalue":
                    float(
                        relative_test.pvalue
                    ),

                "relative_effect_size":
                    relative_effect,

                # Directional evidence
                "n_exiffi_better":
                    int(
                        np.sum(
                            score_difference > 0
                        )
                    ),

                "n_random_better":
                    int(
                        np.sum(
                            score_difference < 0
                        )
                    ),

                "n_equal":
                    int(
                        np.sum(
                            score_difference == 0
                        )
                    ),
            }
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Save paired differences
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[3/5] Saving paired differences..."
    )

    detail_df = pd.concat(
        detail_rows,
        ignore_index=True,
    )

    DETAIL_OUTPUT.parent.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    detail_df.to_csv(
        DETAIL_OUTPUT,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"Saved to:\n{DETAIL_OUTPUT}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Save statistical summary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[4/5] Saving statistical summary..."
    )

    result_df = pd.DataFrame(
        results
    )

    OUTPUT.parent.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    result_df.to_csv(
        OUTPUT,
        index=False,
    )

    print(
        f"Saved to:\n{OUTPUT}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Print results
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[5/5] Statistical results:"
    )

    display_columns = [
        "top_k",
        "n_observations",
        "mean_score_difference",
        "score_difference_ci_lower",
        "score_difference_ci_upper",
        "wilcoxon_score_pvalue",
        "score_effect_size",
        "mean_relative_difference",
        "relative_difference_ci_lower",
        "relative_difference_ci_upper",
        "wilcoxon_relative_pvalue",
        "relative_effect_size",
        "n_exiffi_better",
        "n_random_better",
        "n_equal",
    ]

    print()

    print(
        result_df[
            display_columns
        ].to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nDone."
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
=== FILE: ./scripts/evaluate_exiffi_temporal_drift.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from efd.evaluation.explanation_drift import (
    evaluate_pairwise_drift,
    feature_level_drift,
)


INPUT_DIR = Path(
    "results/explanations"
)

OUTPUT_DIR = Path(
    "results/tables"
)


WINDOWS = [1, 2, 3, 4]


def load_explanation(
    window: int,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, list[str]]:

    path = (
        INPUT_DIR
        / f"static_if_exiffi_w{window}_sample.csv"
    )

    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Explanation file not found: {path}"
        )

    df = pd.read_csv(path)

    importance_cols = [
        column
        for column in df.columns
        if column.startswith("importance__")
    ]

    if not importance_cols:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No importance columns found in {path}"
        )

    feature_names = [
        column.replace(
            "importance__",
            "",
            1,
        )
        for column in importance_cols
    ]

    return df, feature_names


def main():

    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "BASELINE 1 — TEMPORAL EXIFFI "
        "EXPLANATION DRIFT"
    )
    print("=" * 70)

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(
        parents=True,
        exist_ok=True,
    )

    windows = {}

    print("\n[1/5] Loading temporal explanations...")

    for window in WINDOWS:

        df, feature_names = (
            load_explanation(window)
        )

        importance_cols = [
            f"importance__{feature}"
            for feature in feature_names
        ]

        importance = (
            df[importance_cols]
            .to_numpy(dtype=float)
        )

        windows[window] = {
            "df": df,
            "features": feature_names,
            "importance": importance,
        }

        print(
            f"Window {window}: "
            f"{len(df):,} observations, "
            f"{len(feature_names):,} features"
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Pairwise temporal drift
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[2/5] Evaluating pairwise explanation drift..."
    )

    pairwise_rows = []

    comparisons = [
        (1, 2),
        (2, 3),
        (3, 4),
        (1, 3),
        (2, 4),
        (1, 4),
    ]

    for window_a, window_b in comparisons:

        result = evaluate_pairwise_drift(
            importance_a=windows[window_a][
                "importance"
            ],
            importance_b=windows[window_b][
                "importance"
            ],
            window_a=window_a,
            window_b=window_b,
        )

        pairwise_rows.append(
            {
                "window_a": result.window_a,
                "window_b": result.window_b,
                "js_divergence": result.js_divergence,
                "total_variation": result.total_variation,
                "cosine_distance": result.cosine_distance,
                "cosine_similarity": result.cosine_similarity,
                "spearman_correlation": result.spearman_correlation,
                "top_1_overlap": result.top_1_overlap,
                "top_5_overlap": result.top_5_overlap,
                "top_10_overlap": result.top_10_overlap,
                "top_20_overlap": result.top_20_overlap,
                "top_1_jaccard": result.top_1_jaccard,
                "top_5_jaccard": result.top_5_jaccard,
                "top_10_jaccard": result.top_10_jaccard,
                "top_20_jaccard": result.top_20_jaccard,
            }
        )

        print(
            f"Window {window_a} → {window_b}: "
            f"JS={result.js_divergence:.6f}, "
            f"TV={result.total_variation:.6f}, "
            f"Cosine={result.cosine_similarity:.6f}, "
            f"Top-10 Jaccard="
            f"{result.top_10_jaccard:.4f}"
        )

    pairwise_df = pd.DataFrame(
        pairwise_rows
    )

    pairwise_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_drift.csv"
    )

    pairwise_df.to_csv(
        pairwise_path,
        index=False,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Feature-level drift
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[3/5] Evaluating feature-level drift..."
    )

    feature_rows = []

    for window_a, window_b in comparisons:

        drift = feature_level_drift(
            importance_a=windows[window_a][
                "importance"
            ],
            importance_b=windows[window_b][
                "importance"
            ],
            feature_names=windows[window_a][
                "features"
            ],
            window_a=window_a,
            window_b=window_b,
        )

        feature_rows.append(drift)

    feature_df = pd.concat(
        feature_rows,
        ignore_index=True,
    )

    feature_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_feature_drift.csv"
    )

    feature_df.to_csv(
        feature_path,
        index=False,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Top feature stability
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[4/5] Creating top-feature stability..."
    )

    stability_rows = []

    for _, row in pairwise_df.iterrows():

        stability_rows.append(
            {
                "window_a": int(
                    row["window_a"]
                ),
                "window_b": int(
                    row["window_b"]
                ),
                "top_1_overlap": row[
                    "top_1_overlap"
                ],
                "top_5_overlap": row[
                    "top_5_overlap"
                ],
                "top_10_overlap": row[
                    "top_10_overlap"
                ],
                "top_20_overlap": row[
                    "top_20_overlap"
                ],
                "top_1_jaccard": row[
                    "top_1_jaccard"
                ],
                "top_5_jaccard": row[
                    "top_5_jaccard"
                ],
                "top_10_jaccard": row[
                    "top_10_jaccard"
                ],
                "top_20_jaccard": row[
                    "top_20_jaccard"
                ],
            }
        )

    stability_df = pd.DataFrame(
        stability_rows
    )

    stability_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_topk_stability.csv"
    )

    stability_df.to_csv(
        stability_path,
        index=False,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Summary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[5/5] Creating drift summary..."
    )

    summary_rows = []

    for window in WINDOWS:

        df = windows[window]["df"]
        importance = windows[window]["importance"]

        mean_importance = importance.mean(
            axis=0
        )

        total = mean_importance.sum()

        if total > 0:
            distribution = (
                mean_importance / total
            )
        else:
            distribution = mean_importance

        top_1 = pd.Series(
            distribution
        ).nlargest(1).sum()

        top_5 = pd.Series(
            distribution
        ).nlargest(5).sum()

        top_10 = pd.Series(
            distribution
        ).nlargest(10).sum()

        entropy = -(
            distribution[
                distribution > 0
            ]
            * __import__("numpy").log(
                distribution[
                    distribution > 0
                ]
            )
        ).sum()

        effective_features = (
            __import__("numpy").exp(entropy)
        )

        summary_rows.append(
            {
                "window": window,
                "n_observations": len(df),
                "n_features": importance.shape[1],
                "n_fraud": int(
                    df["isFraud"].sum()
                ),
                "fraud_rate": float(
                    df["isFraud"].mean()
                ),
                "top_1_mass": top_1,
                "top_5_mass": top_5,
                "top_10_mass": top_10,
                "entropy": entropy,
                "effective_features": effective_features,
            }
        )

    summary_df = pd.DataFrame(
        summary_rows
    )

    summary_path = (
        OUTPUT_DIR
        / "static_if_exiffi_temporal_drift_summary.csv"
    )

    summary_df.to_csv(
        summary_path,
        index=False,
    )

    print("\nResults saved:")
    print(pairwise_path)
    print(feature_path)
    print(stability_path)
    print(summary_path)

    print(
        "\nPairwise temporal explanation drift:"
    )

    print(
        pairwise_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nTemporal explanation concentration:"
    )

    print(
        summary_df.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nTemporal ExIFFI explanation drift "
        "analysis complete."
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
=== FILE: ./scripts/evaluate_exiffi_temporal_fidelity.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from efd.data.preprocessing import (
    FeaturePolicy,
    NumericTransactionPreprocessor,
)

from efd.data.temporal import (
    chronological_windows,
)

from efd.models.isolation_forest import (
    IsolationForestDetector,
)

from efd.evaluation.explanation_fidelity import (
    evaluate_local_fidelity,
    score_samples_with_feature_names,
)


# ============================================================
# Configuration
# ============================================================

DEFAULT_TRAIN_FRACTION = 0.60
DEFAULT_N_FUTURE_WINDOWS = 4
DEFAULT_N_ESTIMATORS = 300
DEFAULT_RANDOM_STATE = 42
DEFAULT_SAMPLE_SIZE = 100
DEFAULT_SAMPLE_STRATEGY = "stratified"

DEFAULT_INPUT_EXPLANATION_DIR = Path(
    "results/explanations"
)

DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR = Path(
    "results/tables"
)

DEFAULT_K_VALUES = [
    1,
    3,
    5,
    10,
    20,
]

EXPLANATION_FILENAME_TEMPLATE = (
    "static_if_exiffi_w{window}_sample.csv"
)


# ============================================================
# Explanation adapter
# ============================================================


class ExplanationAdapter:
    """
    Minimal adapter that allows an existing ExIFFI
    explanation vector loaded from CSV to be evaluated
    by the existing fidelity implementation.

    The project already stores the normalized ExIFFI
    explanation as:

        importance__<feature>

    Therefore there is no need to regenerate explanations
    for this ticket.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        sample_index: int,
        normalized: np.ndarray,
        transaction_id=None,
    ):
        self.sample_index = int(
            sample_index
        )
        self.transaction_id = transaction_id

        self.normalized = np.asarray(
            normalized,
            dtype=float,
        )


# ============================================================
# Loading explanation files
# ============================================================


def load_explanation_file(
    window: int,
    explanation_dir: Path,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Load the already-generated temporal ExIFFI
    explanation matrix for one future window.
    """

    path = (
        explanation_dir
        / EXPLANATION_FILENAME_TEMPLATE.format(
            window=window
        )
    )

    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "\nMissing temporal explanation file:\n"
            f"  {path}\n\n"
            "Run generate_exiffi_temporal_explanations.py "
            "before running this fidelity experiment."
        )

    df = pd.read_csv(
        path
    )

    # The temporal explanation files currently produced by
    # generate_exiffi_temporal_explanations.py contain a LOCAL
    # sample_index (0..n_sampled-1), not TransactionID.  Therefore
    # TransactionID must not be required here.  The exact sampled
    # observations are reconstructed deterministically from the
    # temporal window using the same sampling strategy and seed.
    required_columns = {
        "sample_index",
        "isFraud",
        "anomaly_score",
    }

    missing = (
        required_columns
        - set(df.columns)
    )

    if missing:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window} explanation file "
            f"is missing required columns: "
            f"{sorted(missing)}"
        )

    importance_columns = [
        column
        for column in df.columns
        if column.startswith(
            "importance__"
        )
    ]

    if not importance_columns:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No ExIFFI importance columns found "
            f"in {path}"
        )

    print(
        f"Window {window}: "
        f"{len(df):,} explanation rows, "
        f"{len(importance_columns):,} features"
    )

    return df


def extract_importance_matrix(
    explanation_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, List[str]]:
    """
    Extract normalized ExIFFI importance vectors from
    the wide-format explanation CSV.
    """

    importance_columns = [
        column
        for column in explanation_df.columns
        if column.startswith(
            "importance__"
        )
    ]

    feature_names = [
        column.replace(
            "importance__",
            "",
            1,
        )
        for column in importance_columns
    ]

    importance_matrix = (
        explanation_df[
            importance_columns
        ]
        .to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )
    )

    return (
        importance_matrix,
        feature_names,
    )


# ============================================================
# Validation
# ============================================================


def validate_explanation_matrix(
    explanation_df: pd.DataFrame,
    importance_matrix: np.ndarray,
    feature_names: List[str],
    expected_feature_names: List[str],
    window: int,
) -> None:
    """
    Validate the explanation matrix before fidelity
    evaluation.
    """

    if (
        feature_names
        != expected_feature_names
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Feature schema mismatch in "
            f"Window {window}.\n"
            "The explanation feature order does not "
            "match the preprocessing/model feature order."
        )

    if importance_matrix.ndim != 2:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window}: importance matrix "
            "must be two-dimensional."
        )

    if importance_matrix.shape[1] != len(
        expected_feature_names
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window}: explanation matrix "
            "contains {importance_matrix.shape[1]} "
            "features but the model expects "
            f"{len(expected_feature_names)}."
        )

    if importance_matrix.shape[0] != len(
        explanation_df
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window}: explanation row count "
            "does not match importance matrix."
        )

    if not np.isfinite(
        importance_matrix
    ).all():
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window}: explanation matrix "
            "contains NaN or infinite values."
        )

    if (
        importance_matrix < -1e-12
    ).any():
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window}: ExIFFI normalized "
            "importance contains negative values."
        )

    sample_indices = explanation_df[
        "sample_index"
    ].to_numpy(dtype=int)

    if len(np.unique(sample_indices)) != len(sample_indices):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window}: duplicate sample_index values found."
        )



# ============================================================
# Sample reconstruction
# ============================================================


def stratified_sample_indices(
    y: pd.Series,
    sample_size: int,
    random_state: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Reproduce the stratified sampling used by
    generate_exiffi_temporal_explanations.py.

    IMPORTANT:
    The generator samples positional rows from the chronological
    window, then shuffles those selected positions.  The explanation
    CSV stores only the resulting local sample_index (0..n-1), so the
    original positions must be reconstructed using this exact procedure.
    """

    if sample_size <= 0:
        raise ValueError("sample_size must be greater than zero.")

    if sample_size > len(y):
        raise ValueError(
            "sample_size cannot exceed the number of observations "
            "in the temporal window."
        )

    rng = np.random.default_rng(random_state)
    y_values = y.to_numpy()
    classes = np.unique(y_values)

    if len(classes) < 2:
        raise ValueError(
            "Stratified sampling requires at least two classes."
        )

    n_classes = len(classes)
    base_per_class = sample_size // n_classes
    remainder = sample_size % n_classes

    selected_indices: list[int] = []

    for class_position, class_value in enumerate(classes):
        class_indices = np.flatnonzero(
            y_values == class_value
        )

        target_n = base_per_class
        if class_position < remainder:
            target_n += 1

        if target_n > len(class_indices):
            target_n = len(class_indices)

        if target_n > 0:
            selected = rng.choice(
                class_indices,
                size=target_n,
                replace=False,
            )
            selected_indices.extend(
                selected.tolist()
            )

    selected_set = set(selected_indices)
    remaining_n = sample_size - len(selected_indices)

    if remaining_n > 0:
        available = np.asarray(
            [
                i
                for i in range(len(y_values))
                if i not in selected_set
            ],
            dtype=int,
        )

        if remaining_n > len(available):
            raise ValueError(
                "Unable to reproduce the requested stratified sample."
            )

        additional = rng.choice(
            available,
            size=remaining_n,
            replace=False,
        )
        selected_indices.extend(
            additional.tolist()
        )

    selected_indices = np.asarray(
        selected_indices,
        dtype=int,
    )

    # This shuffle is part of the generator's sampling algorithm and
    # MUST be retained for exact reconstruction.
    rng.shuffle(selected_indices)

    return selected_indices


def random_sample_indices(
    n_observations: int,
    sample_size: int,
    random_state: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Reproduce the generator's random sampling strategy."""

    if sample_size <= 0:
        raise ValueError("sample_size must be greater than zero.")

    if sample_size > n_observations:
        raise ValueError(
            "sample_size cannot exceed the number of observations "
            "in the temporal window."
        )

    rng = np.random.default_rng(random_state)

    return rng.choice(
        n_observations,
        size=sample_size,
        replace=False,
    )


def sequential_sample_indices(
    n_observations: int,
    sample_size: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Reproduce the generator's sequential sampling strategy."""

    if sample_size <= 0:
        raise ValueError("sample_size must be greater than zero.")

    if sample_size > n_observations:
        raise ValueError(
            "sample_size cannot exceed the number of observations "
            "in the temporal window."
        )

    return np.arange(
        sample_size,
        dtype=int,
    )


def reconstruct_sample_positions(
    window: pd.DataFrame,
    sample_size: int,
    sample_strategy: str,
    random_state: int,
    window_number: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Reproduce the exact positional sample generated for a temporal
    window.

    The explanation generator uses random_state + window_number for
    each window.  This function intentionally mirrors that behaviour.
    """

    seed = random_state + window_number

    if sample_strategy == "stratified":
        return stratified_sample_indices(
            y=window["isFraud"],
            sample_size=sample_size,
            random_state=seed,
        )

    if sample_strategy == "random":
        return random_sample_indices(
            n_observations=len(window),
            sample_size=sample_size,
            random_state=seed,
        )

    if sample_strategy == "sequential":
        return sequential_sample_indices(
            n_observations=len(window),
            sample_size=sample_size,
        )

    raise ValueError(
        f"Unsupported sample strategy: {sample_strategy}. "
        "Expected one of: sequential, random, stratified."
    )


def reconstruct_window_sample(
    window: pd.DataFrame,
    transformed_window: pd.DataFrame,
    explanation_df: pd.DataFrame,
    window_number: int,
    sample_strategy: str,
    random_state: int,
) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """
    Reconstruct the exact sample represented by a temporal ExIFFI file.

    The currently generated explanation files contain:

        sample_index
        isFraud
        anomaly_score
        importance__<feature>

    Here ``sample_index`` is NOT an index into the full temporal window.
    The generator creates ExIFFI explanations with sample_index=i while
    iterating over the already-selected sample.  Consequently, the exact
    source rows must be reproduced from the same sampling configuration.

    TransactionID, when present in the source dataset, is returned for
    auditability, but it is not required in the explanation CSV.
    """

    if "isFraud" not in window.columns:
        raise ValueError(
            "Transaction dataset does not contain 'isFraud'."
        )

    n_explanations = len(explanation_df)

    if n_explanations == 0:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window_number}: explanation file contains no rows."
        )

    sample_indices = explanation_df[
        "sample_index"
    ].to_numpy(dtype=int)

    # The generator assigns sample_index=i in the final sampled order.
    expected_local_indices = np.arange(
        n_explanations,
        dtype=int,
    )

    if not np.array_equal(
        sample_indices,
        expected_local_indices,
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window_number}: sample_index does not represent "
            "the expected local explanation order. Expected 0.."
            f"{n_explanations - 1}, but received "
            f"{sample_indices[:20].tolist()}..."
        )

    if n_explanations > len(window):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window_number}: explanation rows "
            f"({n_explanations}) exceed temporal-window rows "
            f"({len(window)})."
        )

    # Reproduce the exact source-row positions used by the explanation
    # generator.  This is the critical alignment fix.
    source_positions = reconstruct_sample_positions(
        window=window,
        sample_size=n_explanations,
        sample_strategy=sample_strategy,
        random_state=random_state,
        window_number=window_number,
    )

    X_sample = (
        transformed_window
        .iloc[source_positions]
        .to_numpy(dtype=float)
    )

    y_sample = (
        window["isFraud"]
        .iloc[source_positions]
        .to_numpy(dtype=int)
    )

    csv_labels = explanation_df[
        "isFraud"
    ].to_numpy(dtype=int)

    if not np.array_equal(
        y_sample,
        csv_labels,
    ):
        mismatch_positions = np.where(
            y_sample != csv_labels
        )[0]

        details = []
        for position in mismatch_positions[:10]:
            detail = {
                "local_sample_index": int(position),
                "source_window_position": int(
                    source_positions[position]
                ),
                "reconstructed_isFraud": int(
                    y_sample[position]
                ),
                "explanation_isFraud": int(
                    csv_labels[position]
                ),
            }

            if "TransactionID" in window.columns:
                detail["TransactionID"] = window[
                    "TransactionID"
                ].iloc[source_positions[position]]

            details.append(detail)

        raise ValueError(
            f"Window {window_number}: fraud labels reconstructed from "
            "the temporal window do not match the labels stored in the "
            "explanation file.\n"
            f"Sampling strategy used for reconstruction: {sample_strategy}\n"
            f"Random state: {random_state + window_number}\n"
            f"Mismatch count: {len(mismatch_positions)}\n"
            f"First mismatches: {details}\n\n"
            "This normally means the explanation CSV was generated with "
            "a different sampling strategy, sample seed, or version of "
            "the temporal window construction code."
        )

    if "TransactionID" in window.columns:
        transaction_ids = (
            window["TransactionID"]
            .iloc[source_positions]
            .to_numpy()
        )
    else:
        # Keep the output schema stable even for datasets without an ID.
        transaction_ids = np.arange(
            len(source_positions),
            dtype=int,
        )

    print(
        f"  Reconstructed sample using {sample_strategy} sampling "
        f"with seed {random_state + window_number}."
    )

    return (
        X_sample,
        y_sample,
        transaction_ids,
    )


# ============================================================
# Score consistency
# ============================================================


def validate_original_scores(
    model,
    X_sample: np.ndarray,
    explanation_df: pd.DataFrame,
    tolerance: float = 1e-8,
) -> None:
    """
    Verify that the model's current score agrees with
    the anomaly score stored when the explanations were
    generated.

    The project convention is:

        sklearn score_samples:
            larger = more normal

        research anomaly_score:
            -score_samples
    """

    model_scores = score_samples_with_feature_names(
        model,
        X_sample,
    )

    expected_anomaly_scores = (
        -model_scores
    )

    stored_anomaly_scores = (
        explanation_df[
            "anomaly_score"
        ]
        .to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )
    )

    if not np.allclose(
        expected_anomaly_scores,
        stored_anomaly_scores,
        rtol=tolerance,
        atol=tolerance,
    ):
        maximum_difference = float(
            np.max(
                np.abs(
                    expected_anomaly_scores
                    - stored_anomaly_scores
                )
            )
        )

        raise ValueError(
            "Stored ExIFFI anomaly scores do not "
            "match the reconstructed Isolation Forest "
            "scores.\n"
            f"Maximum absolute difference: "
            f"{maximum_difference:.12g}"
        )


# ============================================================
# Fidelity evaluation
# ============================================================


def evaluate_window_fidelity(
    window: int,
    model,
    X_sample: np.ndarray,
    y_sample: np.ndarray,
    transaction_ids: np.ndarray,
    explanation_df: pd.DataFrame,
    importance_matrix: np.ndarray,
    baseline: np.ndarray,
    feature_names: List[str],
    k_values: List[int],
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Evaluate perturbation fidelity for one temporal window.
    """

    explanations = [
        ExplanationAdapter(
            sample_index=int(
                explanation_df.iloc[i][
                    "sample_index"
                ]
            ),
            normalized=importance_matrix[i],
            transaction_id=transaction_ids[i],
        )
        for i in range(
            len(explanation_df)
        )
    ]

    rows = []

    for i, explanation in enumerate(
        explanations
    ):

        x = X_sample[i]

        for k in k_values:

            if k > X_sample.shape[1]:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"k={k} exceeds the number of "
                    f"features ({X_sample.shape[1]})."
                )

            result = evaluate_local_fidelity(
                model=model,
                x=x,
                explanation=explanation,
                baseline=baseline,
                k=k,
            )

            rows.append(
                {
                    "window": window,
                    "TransactionID": transaction_ids[i],
                    "sample_index": (
                        explanation.sample_index
                    ),
                    "isFraud": int(
                        y_sample[i]
                    ),
                    "class": (
                        "fraud"
                        if int(y_sample[i]) == 1
                        else "nonfraud"
                    ),
                    "top_k": int(k),
                    "top_k_mass": (
                        result.top_k_mass
                    ),
                    "baseline_score": (
                        result.baseline_score
                    ),
                    "perturbed_score": (
                        result.perturbed_score
                    ),
                    "score_change": (
                        result.score_change
                    ),
                    "absolute_score_change": abs(
                        result.score_change
                    ),
                    "relative_score_change": (
                        result.relative_score_change
                    ),
                    "n_features": (
                        len(feature_names)
                    ),
                }
            )

    return pd.DataFrame(
        rows
    )


# ============================================================
# Summary helpers
# ============================================================


def build_summary(
    fidelity_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Build overall temporal fidelity summary.
    """

    summary = (
        fidelity_df
        .groupby(
            [
                "window",
                "top_k",
            ],
            as_index=False,
        )
        .agg(
            n_observations=(
                "sample_index",
                "nunique",
            ),
            mean_top_k_mass=(
                "top_k_mass",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_top_k_mass=(
                "top_k_mass",
                "median",
            ),
            mean_score_change=(
                "score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_score_change=(
                "score_change",
                "median",
            ),
            mean_absolute_score_change=(
                "absolute_score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_absolute_score_change=(
                "absolute_score_change",
                "median",
            ),
            mean_relative_score_change=(
                "relative_score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_relative_score_change=(
                "relative_score_change",
                "median",
            ),
        )
        .sort_values(
            [
                "window",
                "top_k",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(
            drop=True
        )
    )

    return summary


def build_class_summary(
    fidelity_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Build fraud/non-fraud temporal fidelity summary.
    """

    summary = (
        fidelity_df
        .groupby(
            [
                "window",
                "isFraud",
                "class",
                "top_k",
            ],
            as_index=False,
        )
        .agg(
            n_observations=(
                "sample_index",
                "nunique",
            ),
            mean_top_k_mass=(
                "top_k_mass",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_top_k_mass=(
                "top_k_mass",
                "median",
            ),
            mean_score_change=(
                "score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_score_change=(
                "score_change",
                "median",
            ),
            mean_absolute_score_change=(
                "absolute_score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_absolute_score_change=(
                "absolute_score_change",
                "median",
            ),
            mean_relative_score_change=(
                "relative_score_change",
                "mean",
            ),
            median_relative_score_change=(
                "relative_score_change",
                "median",
            ),
        )
        .sort_values(
            [
                "window",
                "isFraud",
                "top_k",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(
            drop=True
        )
    )

    return summary


# ============================================================
# Temporal fidelity trajectory
# ============================================================


def build_fidelity_trajectory(
    summary_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Calculate temporal changes in the primary fidelity
    measure.

    The primary fidelity measure remains:

        mean_relative_score_change

    Additional quantities:

        change_from_previous_window
        absolute_change_from_previous_window
        change_from_window_1
        absolute_change_from_window_1
    """

    rows = []

    for top_k, group in summary_df.groupby(
        "top_k"
    ):

        group = (
            group
            .sort_values("window")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        baseline_value = float(
            group.iloc[0][
                "mean_relative_score_change"
            ]
        )

        previous_value = None

        for _, row in group.iterrows():

            current_value = float(
                row[
                    "mean_relative_score_change"
                ]
            )

            if previous_value is None:

                change_previous = 0.0
                absolute_change_previous = 0.0

            else:

                change_previous = (
                    current_value
                    - previous_value
                )

                absolute_change_previous = abs(
                    change_previous
                )

            change_window_1 = (
                current_value
                - baseline_value
            )

            rows.append(
                {
                    "window": int(
                        row["window"]
                    ),
                    "top_k": int(top_k),
                    "mean_relative_score_change": (
                        current_value
                    ),
                    "window_to_window_change": (
                        change_previous
                    ),
                    "absolute_window_to_window_change": (
                        absolute_change_previous
                    ),
                    "change_from_window_1": (
                        change_window_1
                    ),
                    "absolute_change_from_window_1": (
                        abs(change_window_1)
                    ),
                }
            )

            previous_value = (
                current_value
            )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(rows)
        .sort_values(
            [
                "top_k",
                "window",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# Class trajectory
# ============================================================


def build_class_trajectory(
    class_summary_df: pd.DataFrame,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """
    Build class-specific fidelity trajectories.
    """

    rows = []

    for (
        fraud_value,
        class_name,
        top_k,
    ), group in class_summary_df.groupby(
        [
            "isFraud",
            "class",
            "top_k",
        ]
    ):

        group = (
            group
            .sort_values("window")
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        baseline_value = float(
            group.iloc[0][
                "mean_relative_score_change"
            ]
        )

        previous_value = None

        for _, row in group.iterrows():

            current_value = float(
                row[
                    "mean_relative_score_change"
                ]
            )

            if previous_value is None:

                change_previous = 0.0

            else:

                change_previous = (
                    current_value
                    - previous_value
                )

            rows.append(
                {
                    "window": int(
                        row["window"]
                    ),
                    "isFraud": int(
                        fraud_value
                    ),
                    "class": class_name,
                    "top_k": int(top_k),
                    "mean_relative_score_change": (
                        current_value
                    ),
                    "window_to_window_change": (
                        change_previous
                    ),
                    "change_from_window_1": (
                        current_value
                        - baseline_value
                    ),
                }
            )

            previous_value = (
                current_value
            )

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(rows)
        .sort_values(
            [
                "class",
                "top_k",
                "window",
            ]
        )
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# Main experiment
# ============================================================


def run_experiment(
    transactions_path: str,
    explanation_dir: Path = DEFAULT_INPUT_EXPLANATION_DIR,
    output_dir: Path = DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR,
    train_fraction: float = DEFAULT_TRAIN_FRACTION,
    n_future_windows: int = DEFAULT_N_FUTURE_WINDOWS,
    n_estimators: int = DEFAULT_N_ESTIMATORS,
    random_state: int = DEFAULT_RANDOM_STATE,
    k_values: List[int] | None = None,
    sample_strategy: str = DEFAULT_SAMPLE_STRATEGY,
):
    """
    Evaluate ExIFFI perturbation fidelity across all
    chronological future windows.

    The Isolation Forest and preprocessing are fitted
    once on the training period.

    Existing temporal ExIFFI explanation files are then
    loaded and evaluated against that same static model.
    """

    if k_values is None:
        k_values = list(
            DEFAULT_K_VALUES
        )

    print("=" * 70)
    print(
        "BASELINE 1 — TEMPORAL EXIFFI "
        "EXPLANATION FIDELITY"
    )
    print("=" * 70)

    print(
        f"\nTraining fraction: "
        f"{train_fraction}"
    )

    print(
        f"Future windows: "
        f"{n_future_windows}"
    )

    print(
        f"Isolation Forest trees: "
        f"{n_estimators}"
    )

    print(
        f"Random state: "
        f"{random_state}"
    )

    print(
        f"Top-k values: "
        f"{k_values}"
    )

    print(
        f"Sample strategy: "
        f"{sample_strategy}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Load temporal explanation matrices
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[1/8] Loading temporal explanations..."
    )

    explanation_windows: Dict[
        int,
        pd.DataFrame,
    ] = {}

    importance_matrices: Dict[
        int,
        np.ndarray,
    ] = {}

    explanation_feature_names: Dict[
        int,
        List[str],
    ] = {}

    for window_number in range(
        1,
        n_future_windows + 1,
    ):

        explanation_df = (
            load_explanation_file(
                window=window_number,
                explanation_dir=explanation_dir,
            )
        )

        (
            importance_matrix,
            feature_names,
        ) = extract_importance_matrix(
            explanation_df
        )

        explanation_windows[
            window_number
        ] = explanation_df

        importance_matrices[
            window_number
        ] = importance_matrix

        explanation_feature_names[
            window_number
        ] = feature_names

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Load data
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[2/8] Loading transaction dataset..."
    )

    transactions = pd.read_csv(
        transactions_path,
        low_memory=False,
    )

    print(
        f"Loaded {len(transactions):,} "
        "transactions."
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Chronological split
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[3/8] Creating chronological windows..."
    )

    train, future_windows = chronological_windows(
        transactions,
        time_col="TransactionDT",
        train_fraction=train_fraction,
        n_future_windows=n_future_windows,
    )

    print(
        f"Training observations: "
        f"{len(train):,}"
    )

    for i, window in enumerate(
        future_windows,
        start=1,
    ):

        print(
            f"Window {i}: "
            f"{len(window):,} observations"
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Fit training-only preprocessing
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[4/8] Fitting training-only preprocessing..."
    )

    preprocessor = NumericTransactionPreprocessor(
        policy=FeaturePolicy(
            target="isFraud",
            temporal="TransactionDT",
            identifier="TransactionID",
            max_missing_rate=0.95,
        )
    )

    x_train = (
        preprocessor
        .fit_transform(train)
    )

    feature_names = (
        preprocessor.feature_names_
    )

    print(
        f"Selected features: "
        f"{len(feature_names)}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Validate all explanation feature schemas.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    for window_number in range(
        1,
        n_future_windows + 1,
    ):

        validate_explanation_matrix(
            explanation_df=(
                explanation_windows[
                    window_number
                ]
            ),
            importance_matrix=(
                importance_matrices[
                    window_number
                ]
            ),
            feature_names=(
                explanation_feature_names[
                    window_number
                ]
            ),
            expected_feature_names=feature_names,
            window=window_number,
        )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Train static Isolation Forest
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[5/8] Training static Isolation Forest..."
    )

    detector = IsolationForestDetector(
        n_estimators=n_estimators,
        max_samples="auto",
        contamination="auto",
        random_state=random_state,
        n_jobs=-1,
    )

    detector.fit(
        x_train
    )

    print(
        f"Isolation Forest trained with "
        f"{n_estimators} trees."
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Training-only median baseline
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[6/8] Computing training-only "
        "median intervention baseline..."
    )

    baseline = np.median(
        x_train.to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        ),
        axis=0,
    )

    if baseline.shape != (
        len(feature_names),
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Training baseline does not contain "
            "one value per selected feature."
        )

    print(
        f"Baseline features: "
        f"{len(baseline)}"
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Temporal fidelity evaluation
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "\n[7/8] Evaluating temporal fidelity..."
    )

    all_results = []

    for window_number, window in enumerate(
        future_windows,
        start=1,
    ):

        print()
        print(
            "-" * 70
        )

        print(
            f"Window {window_number}"
        )

        explanation_df = (
            explanation_windows[
                window_number
            ]
        )

        importance_matrix = (
            importance_matrices[
                window_number
            ]
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Transform using SAME training-fitted preprocessor.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        X_window = (
            preprocessor
            .transform(window)
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Reconstruct the exact sample represented by the
        # explanation CSV.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        (
            X_sample,
            y_sample,
            transaction_ids,
        ) = reconstruct_window_sample(
            window=window,
            transformed_window=X_window,
            explanation_df=explanation_df,
            window_number=window_number,
            sample_strategy=sample_strategy,
            random_state=random_state,
        )

        print(
            f"  Observations evaluated: "
            f"{len(X_sample)}"
        )

        print(
            f"  Features: "
            f"{X_sample.shape[1]}"
        )

        print(
            f"  Fraud: "
            f"{int(y_sample.sum())}"
        )

        print(
            f"  Non-fraud: "
            f"{int(len(y_sample) - y_sample.sum())}"
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Ensure the model scores match the scores that were
        # present when ExIFFI explanations were generated.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        validate_original_scores(
            model=detector.model,
            X_sample=X_sample,
            explanation_df=explanation_df,
        )

        print(
            "  Original model scores: validated"
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Evaluate fidelity.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        window_results = (
            evaluate_window_fidelity(
                window=window_number,
                model=detector.model,
                X_sample=X_sample,
                y_sample=y_sample,
                transaction_ids=transaction_ids,
                explanation_df=explanation_df,
                importance_matrix=importance_matrix,
                baseline=baseline,
                feature_names=feature_names,
                k_values=k_values,
            )
        )

        all_results.append(
            window_results
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # Print quick window summary.
        # -----------------------------------------------------

        window_summary = (
            window_results
            .groupby("top_k")
            .agg(
                mean_top_k_mass=(
                    "top_k_mass",
                    "mean",
                ),
                mean_relative_score_change=(
                    "relative_score_change",
                    "mean",
                ),
            )
            .reset_index()
        )

        print(
            "\n  Fidelity:"
        )

        print(
            window_summary.to_string(
                index=False
            )
        )

    fidelity_df = pd.concat(
        all_results,
        ignore_index=True,
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Summaries and saving
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    print(
